- **Shop chests cannot be broken.** Deleting the shop is what turns the chest back into an ordinary, breakable chest - so there is always a way out for the owner, and never one for a thief.
- **DELETE SHOP is in the owner GUI**, behind a two-click confirm that shows what is about to be lost (prices and settings - never the chest or its contents). `/chestshop toggle` and `/chestshop delete [confirm]` do the same from chat.
- **`shop.lifecycle`** proves the whole thing headlessly: it places a real chest, sneak-right-clicks it with a real Buy Sign through the real dispatch, and asserts the shop was recorded under the right owner; cycles buy → sell → closed and back, asserting a closed shop really stops trading and loses its claim exemptions; asserts a live shop chest is protected from breaking; and asserts deleting leaves a breakable chest behind. Verified to bite: forcing the sneak click to pass fails the build.

## 1.0.50

### 💥 Explosion Rebuild now covers the wreckage a boss makes
- **Boss craters used to land in a private list.** The wither's slam and nuke craters are carved one block at a time by `WitherReworkManager`, which kept its own `removeBlock` queue - invisible to everything else. So Explosion Rebuild covered TNT and `level.explode(...)`, but the wreckage a boss actually made rode a second, hidden queue. Boss carving now goes through the *same* rebuild queue as an explosion: same delay, same config switch, same restore path, same frozen columns - and a crash mid-fight cannot strand it any more.
- **A bare `/explosionrebuild` used to *flip the switch*.** Running it just to read the setting silently turned the feature off, which is exactly how "explosion rebuild is broken" gets reported. It now reports: ON or OFF, what triggers a rebuild, that it never touches claims, and how many blocks are queued right now. `/raidcooldown` and `/NiceKeepInventory claimother` had the same trap and now report too; `on` / `off` are the only things that change anything.
- **Proven, not assumed.** `ffSelfTest` gained `explosion.rebuild-boss-source`: it builds a stone shelf in the air, detonates a real `level.explode(zombie, ...)` next to it, and asserts that every block the blast removed was queued, was put back, that the queue drained, and that **no item entity dropped** (the duplication bug). It then runs a real wither carve and asserts that one queues and restores too. The build now fails if boss wreckage ever stops rebuilding again.

### 💥 Explosion Rebuild, take two - it was off, and my first proof was too weak
- **It defaulted to OFF.** A server that never opened the config had no blast recovery at all, so TNT, creepers, blood revenants and every kind of nuke left permanent holes. It is now **ON unless an admin turns it off** (matching raid cooldown and the wither rework), and if a config file explicitly says otherwise, startup logs one clear line saying so, with the command to change it.
- **My previous test proved the wrong half.** It used a test-only flush that skips the five-second delay, so it never exercised the code that actually runs in game. The check now ages the queue and then calls the **real tick**, and separately asserts that a blast is *not* queued while the feature is off - so a queue that captures but never drains now fails the build.
- If your `config.json` still says `explosion_rebuild: false` (a very likely leftover from the bare command that used to flip the switch), run `/explosionrebuild on` once - it will report the state before and after.

### 🤖 Duel bots no longer hit you through the ceiling
- **Their reach was 2D.** A bot's mace leap is a horizontal arc, and the landing smash was gated on horizontal distance alone - so a bot standing on the ground connected with a player eight blocks above it. The smash and the leap start now both check the vertical gap too, which is what a real player's reach does.
- **The mace smash was being eaten by the light-attack pacing window.** A single heavy falling hit was treated like the third click of a spam combo and discarded. A falling mace swing now always lands.

### 💨 Sonic Boom actually fires now
- **The trigger was too strict to ever register.** It demanded more than 1.7 blocks/tick *and* a standing elytra, but a firework glide sits right at that edge - which is exactly why it never seemed to work. Charging now starts above 0.5 blocks/tick of gliding and completes in 14 ticks (8 for follow-ups).
- **No more 60-second lockout.** The rule is now *once per flight*: after a boom, folding your wings or landing re-arms it, so a long trip can boom again and again - just never twice inside one glide. A 1.5s guard stops a double-fire.
- **It is genuinely faster than normal.** The launch now holds you at 3.4 blocks/tick along your look direction for about a second (a boosted firework glide is ~1.5-2.0), keeping a quarter of your old momentum so it cannot be aimed backwards, and it flags the client so the speed actually sticks. You get an action-bar call when it fires and when your wings re-arm it.

### 🎟 The lottery now gets bigger the more people play
- **A crowd bonus on top of the pot.** Every ticket adds to the pot as before, and the house now also pays a participation bonus: $25 per ticket sold, $2,500 per *different* player holding one, plus a squared crowd term - so the same 40 tickets from 8 players are worth far more than 40 tickets from one whale.
- **You can see the maths.** The lottery window shows the pot, the live jackpot, the crowd bonus, how many players are in, what a purchase does to the jackpot, and the exact cost of each ticket. The draw announces the crowd bonus separately.
- **`/lottery` is reachable from `/menu`.** The hub button was already there and now shows the pot, the jackpot, the players-in count and your own tickets; `/ff help menu` lists the lottery (and the bank, gems, expeditions, cosmetics, recipes and leaderboard) instead of pretending the menu is only the first half of itself.

### ⏳ The Time Lord is a proper `/raidboss` entry
- `/raidboss help` lists him, `/raidboss help timelord` (or `time_lord`, `rift`, `spacetime`) opens a recipe page in the GUI, and the boss list has his token at the end of the row - with the real recipe (4 Clocks + 4 Echo Shards ringing an Amethyst Shard), what he does, and what he drops. The endgame hint that used to occupy that slot moved into the info book.
- **The Elder Warden was in the help GUI but not in the command.** `/raidboss help sculk` answered "Unknown boss" even though the GUI had a page for it. It is a listed boss now, with a console page of its own.
- **One list of boss names, used by both paths.** The in-game lookup and the console fallback had two hand-written alias chains that could (and did) drift apart. They now share a single table, and an unknown boss returns a failing command result instead of a silent success - so a script or command block can tell the difference.
- **Locked in by `commands.raidboss.pages`.** Every canonical boss - `king`, `slime`, `golem`, `mindbinder`, `snow`, `sculk`, `timelord` - is resolved through the *real* command, both in game and from console, plus every alias the command advertises, and the canonical list is deliberately kept separate from the command's own table: dropping a boss from that table fails the build instead of quietly dropping out of the test with it. (That last part was a genuine first-attempt bug in the check: iterating the very list it validates made a removal invisible.)

### ✅ Two more headless gates, both verified to bite
- **`admin.toggles.report-not-flip`** runs the bare admin toggles (`/explosionrebuild`, `/raidcooldown`, `/NiceKeepInventory claimother`) through the real dispatcher and fails the build if any of them changes a setting. Checked by re-introducing the flip and watching it fail with `bare admin command(s) changed a setting instead of reporting it: [explosionrebuild]`.

### ⛏ The guild mine says what everything costs
- Every tier card now states its **cost, what it pays per interval and per hour**, what the upgrade gains you over your current mine, and whether you can afford it - with your balance and the shortfall right there on the card instead of only in chat after a failed click.
- The info book shows your balance, what this mine earns an hour, and exactly **how much more stock is needed** before the next payout, plus a plain-language reminder that the mine fills itself and deposits are optional.
- Clicking a tier you cannot build now **tells you why** (already built, or build the previous level first) instead of silently doing nothing, and a successful build confirms what you just bought and what it pays.

## 1.0.49

### ⏳ The Time Lord - a new raid boss, summoned by the **Space-Time Rift**
- **Craft the Space-Time Rift** from 4 Clocks + 4 Echo Shards ringing an Amethyst Shard. Right-click it and the air **tears open** - a vertical rift of portal sparks and end rods - and the Time Lord rises out of it.
- **He actually stops time.** The world freezes for real (`setFrozen` on the server tick rate) - mobs, redstone, everything - while the fighter is locked in place, mid-air if the stop caught them jumping. He taunts, then time resumes. That is the one dangerous API in the fight, so it is fenced four ways: every stop is bounded and has a wall-clock deadline, he only ever unfreezes what he froze, a daemon thread queues the release so the world thaws even if the tick handler stops, and the freeze is a no-op during the self-test.
- **Abilities**: safe **blink** (ray-traced, never into a wall, lava or the void, capped at 12 per fight so he cannot teleport-spam), **rift volleys** that fling random junk through tears in the air (the arrows carry the junk as their pickup item, so a survivable volley literally rains random objects), and a **halo of five arrows** that appear around you and snap inward one at a time.
- **Phase 2 - the grand stop.** At a third health he holds one long stop, rings you with 24 arrows drawn in the frozen air, tells you *"I will hold this moment forever,"* then resumes time and fires the entire ring at once.
- **Custom death.** The killing blow is cancelled: he tries to stop time one last time, the rift opens, flickers, and refuses to hold - *"My power... it is not... held..."* - and the moment takes him. He always drops a **Time Lord Loot Box** (two in phase 2).

### 🕰 The three Time Lord legendaries (Loot Box)
- **Pocket-Watch** - right-click to stop time for everything around you (5s, Version II 10s). **Sneak-right-click with a Netherite Ingot in your off hand to reforge it to Version II.** Long cooldown: 3 min, or 5 min for II. This one deliberately does *not* use the global tick freeze - a server-wide stop cannot exempt its own wearer, so it would freeze you too and do nothing. It suspends every other creature individually instead, so you keep acting.
- **Chrono Shard** - rewinds you to where you stood five seconds ago *and* to the health you had then, so getting knocked from 20 hearts to 8 can be undone. 60s cooldown.
- **Hourglass of Haste** - 8s of Speed II, Haste II and Jump Boost, then the bill comes due: 3s of Slowness II. 90s cooldown.

### 🧬 New enchantment: **Aging**
- Every hit ages the target. At **5 stacks** the years catch up at once - a burst of damage plus Slowness - and the stacks are spent. Stacks decay after 6 seconds. **Bosses cap at 4 stacks**, so a boss can never be cheese-loop-burst by an infinite stack engine. Drops from the Time Lord and spins in his loot box.

### 🔧 Fixes
- **Every spawner was recorded as owned by the literal name "unbound".** The display fallback (`spawnerOwnerName` returns `"unbound"` when no name is stored) was being read by the record-writing path, so a spawner item carrying an owner UUID but no name re-bound itself with the placeholder - and the placeholder became the real owner name, permanently. The display form and the stored form are now separate methods, records are self-healed on load (owner known, name missing), and names are kept in a cache inside `spawners.json` that refreshes from the online players, so the repair survives restarts and works for owners who are offline.
- **`/ff restore` opened the wrong screen** - the soulbound/snapshot `RestoreMenu` instead of the recovery preview-and-confirm GUI. Bare `/ff restore` now opens the recovery GUI (in-game) or prints its preview (console), `/ff restore confirm` runs it, and the old screen moved to an explicit `/ff restore self`.
- **Backpack and bundle contents could be wiped by dying.** Contents were only written back to the item when the menu closed, so the item in your inventory was stale the whole time the menu was open - dying (where the death path copies the item before the menu closes) saved a pack missing everything added since it was opened. Both menus now persist on every content change, making the item the single source of truth.
- **The Wormhole Potion was a flat black blob.** A potion item renders from its `potion_contents`; without it the client has no colour or overlay to draw. It now carries a purple custom colour on a water base (no stray effects - the warp is the mod's own logic).
- **`/ff boss wipe`** - ends every tracked fight now: the Time Lord's boss is discarded, his bar removed and any frozen tick rate **released**, then all remaining boss bars are cleared. Unlike `/ff boss clear`, which only forgets the bars.

### 🔨 The Item Forge could not upgrade anything it was supposed to
- **Fixed: no armour or weapon legendary could be upgraded at all.** The forge checked rune fusion *before* the upgrade branch, and "socketable" means any weapon or armour piece - so a Slime Boots (or a Wither Crown, a Glacier Cloak, a Warlord's Axe...) with its set's upgrade material in the material slot was read as a botched rune attempt and refused with *"Weapons and armor take a Rune in the material slot"*. It now recognises its own upgrade materials and falls through to the upgrade, and rune fusion on a legendary still works when the material really is a rune.
- **Forge legendaries also shift-click into the forge again.** They were only routed to the target slot if they were "enchantable targets", which the unbreakable ones (Slime Boots) are not, so the only way in was dragging by hand.
- **Locked in by a new gate.** `ffSelfTest` now carries `forge.legendary-upgrades`: every upgradable legendary is forged through the real API from its own tier up to **Tier III** with its own material, and the build fails if any of them comes back empty. That check is what turned this from a guess into a proof - and it immediately flagged a second thing, the *Multidimensional Army* being handed out at Tier III (which is intended, and now documented in the check).

### ⚔ Enchantments the mod means to have
- **Impaling hits anything in water.** Vanilla's condition was `#minecraft:sensitive_to_impaling` (aquatic mobs only), so impaling a drowned that was standing in a lake did nothing. The condition is now "the target is in water", and Impaling can also go on swords and the mod's melee weapons - not just tridents.
- **Infinity works on crossbows**, and **Breach works on swords and axes** as well as maces.
- **Protection types combine.** Vanilla makes Protection, Fire Protection, Blast Protection and Projectile Protection mutually exclusive; `exclusive_set/armor` is now empty, so one chestplate can carry all four. (Damage enchantments - Sharpness, Smite, Bane, Breach - were already freed the same way.)
- These are datapack overrides shipping as `data/minecraft/enchantment/*.json` plus the mod's own item tags; a wrong tag reference makes the whole enchantment fail to load, which is exactly what the first attempt did until the tags were added and the datapack was verified loading clean.

### 🔧 Fixes found while building him
- **The Wither Loot Box rendered as the King's box.** Both are chest items and both claimed `custom_model_data 4550005`, so the resource pack could only ever resolve one of them. The Wither box now has its own id and its own ashen texture.
- **The Elder Warden Loot Box had no model entry at all** - it rendered as a plain chest. Added to the chest definition.
- **Loot boxes could be placed as real chest blocks.** `isNonPlaceable` was missing four of the boxes (including the Wither, Elder Warden and Time Lord boxes), which would have turned a box into an unreachable chest block.
- **The other loot boxes and the Upwards Hopper were missing from `/ff give`.** Four handled item types had no catalog entry at all, so an admin could not hand them out.
- **Recipes can no longer produce a different item than the code expects.** `ffAuditSources` now cross-checks every crafting recipe's `custom_data.ff` type and `custom_model_data` number against `ModItems.java`. If they drift, the crafted stack is an item no code recognises - which is exactly how a craftable token ends up "not working".
- **The item right-click surface is now asserted, not eyeballed.** The self-test requires every custom item type in the `/ff give` catalog to be either registered with a right-click handler or explicitly declared passive, so a new item cannot ship as a dead item that does nothing. A second gate compares the declared predicate list against the predicates `onUseItem` actually tests, so the table cannot drift out of step with the dispatch chain.
- **The self-test now literally clicks every item.** `items.use.invoke` drives the real right-click chain on a headless player (Fabric's fake player, which carries a dummy connection so chat and menus do not throw) and fails the build for any item whose click nothing claims. It does not assume one shape of "right-click": it tries a plain click, a sneak click, a click with a **socketable in the other hand** (a rune clicked bare is *supposed* to do nothing), and the worn-gear poses - **worn in each armour slot, empty hand, sneak** and **empty-hand sneak right-click on a block** - because the mask, shroud, sculk sense and Warlord's Rage fire from the armour slot through the block path, not from the hand. An item only passes by resolving the click or by being listed as passive or as deliberately deferring to vanilla (the two bows, whose right-click is the vanilla draw). Current result: **55 items invoked - 44 acted, 9 refused with their own reason (cooldown / condition, which is a working handler), 2 defer to vanilla, 0 dead, 0 threw.** Everything the probe summons or drops is swept afterwards - including ending any fight the Space-Time Rift started and releasing the time stop - so the check never leaves the world it measures changed.
- **Two items were lying about having a right-click.** *Warlord's Axe* (its Rage is on-hit and per-tick) and the *Raiders Item Upgrader* (an Item Forge material, "NOT a block") sat in the handler table claiming a click that nothing implemented - which is exactly how a "handled" item hides a dead one. Both are now declared passive, and the new probe is what caught them. It also proved itself: with the Hourglass dispatch temporarily neutered, the gate failed with `items.use.invoke -> item(s) did nothing when right-clicked: [hourglass_of_haste]`.
- **`ffAuditSources` now scrubs iCloud conflict copies first.** A resurrected `chair 2.json` inside `src/` used to fail the audit for the wrong reason.
- **The Vanilla clock dial is preserved.** The Clock's item definition dispatches on the in-game time; the override embeds the vanilla definition verbatim as its fallback, so ordinary clocks keep their dial. The overrides for all four new base items are generated by `tools/make_item_definitions.py`, and the new textures by `tools/make_time_lord_textures.py`, so the art and the dispatch tables are reproducible.
- **The bundled resource pack was rebuilt** (`fortuneandfavors-resourcepack.zip`) with the 14 new files (5 models, 6 textures, 2 item definitions).

## 1.0.48

### 🧪 The mod now tests itself (run this before every release)
- **`./gradlew ffAuditSources`** - a second-long static audit, wired into `check` so it also gates `build`. It fails on: any shipped datapack JSON that does not parse, any crafting recipe whose recipe-book **unlock advancement** is missing or vice versa (the recipe would be invisible in the book), any **menu class that no code opens** (an unreachable feature), and any command `/ff help` advertises that is not actually registered.
- **`./gradlew ffSelfTest`** - boots a headless dev server, asserts the runtime surface, writes `build/ff-selftest.json`, and **fails the build** if anything is broken. 16 checks: every item in the `/ff give` catalog (real, named, unique alias - no unreachable or ambiguous items), every **claim / spawner / machine recovery path** (the preview counts one line per recoverable record, a seeded record is detected and then the file is restored byte-for-byte, and applying twice merges nothing the second time), data-file integrity plus "writes are enabled" for every manager, the join-time **registry fingerprint** (non-empty and stable, so it can never reject a legitimate join), the documented command surface (and no registered command that is an empty shell), and every recipe unlock actually loading.
- **Removed a dead menu.** `ForgeMenu` (the old "Boss Forge", superseded by the placeable Item Forge) still compiled but nothing had opened it for several versions - 305 lines of a feature that looked present and was not. The audit task now fails if any menu becomes unreachable again.

### 🎵 Backpack Jukebox now actually plays
- **Fixed the silent jukebox.** The music was being killed on the very next tick whenever the pack carrying the disc was in the **off hand** (or open in a menu) - the "is it still there?" check only ever looked at the main inventory. Hands, the off hand and the open backpack menu are all checked now, and a short grace window absorbs transient states so the song survives menu swaps and dimension travel.
- **No more silent failures**: the server sends the track as a **sound-event id** the client resolves from the plain sound registry (the old datapack song lookup could fail quietly), failures are logged, and the client **re-starts a song that the engine ever drops** instead of going dead.

### ☠ The Wither - a much bigger, louder fight
- **New attack: WITHER NUKE.** It roots itself, telegraphs the blast radius with a growing ground ring, then detonates - a boss-sized explosion, a launch shockwave out to 22 blocks, heavy wither and a lingering hazard pool in the crater.
- **Five more moves**: **Soul Nova** (a 360° ring of skulls), **Skull Rain** (skulls called down onto every nearby player, with a marked impact zone), **Wither Drain** (a sustained soul-fire beam that damages, withers and heals the boss), **Soul Pull** (reels players in, then bursts) and **Shred Blink** (teleports behind its target and bites). Ability cooldowns were tightened so it cycles faster.
- **Better VFX everywhere**: a permanent soul-fire/smoke aura (thicker in phase 2), a rotating purple ring once supercharged, a bigger arrival show (expanding rings + a harmless lightning crown), a longer death ceremony with a rising spirit spiral and lightning at the star, and lightning around the body.
- **Normal wither name**: the entity is just **"Wither"** ("Wither King" when promoted) - the Ascended branding only appears in the boss bar and announcements. Fight identity is now tracked with **entity tags** instead of parsing the display name (which used to re-adopt a reloaded fight as a KING).
- **Supercharge it with a Trophy**: offering a **boss trophy** (King Wither Skeleton Trophy, Boss Trophy, ...) mid-fight supercharges it - +25% max HP, more armor, more speed, shorter cooldowns, at least 60% health back, a purple aura, lightning rings and a purple boss bar. The **Wither Skeleton Skull** king promotion is unchanged (summon window only).

### 🎁 Wither Loot Box
- The Wither now **drops its own Wither Loot Box** (two for the King). It spins the wither legendary pool - **the three wither legendaries: Wither Skeleton Blade, Crown and Staff** - with its own menu title, and `/ff give witherloot` hands one out for testing.

### ⛏️ Excavator is honest now
- **Excavator double-drops and auto-smelt only fire on naturally generated blocks** - placing a block and re-mining it no longer farms free perk procs.

## 1.0.47

### ⬆ Upwards Hopper (exclusive shop, $7,500)
- **A shop-only machine with NO crafting recipe**: looks like a hopper, behaves the opposite. It **pushes items UP** into the container above, pulls **down** from the container it points into, and overflows **side-to-side like a normal hopper**. Two stacked upwards hoppers circulate instead of jamming.
- Right-click it for its info/effects; sneak-right-click to pick it up like any machine.

### 🪧 Chest shops modernized
- **Buy & sell signs now glow** with enchanted glint, richer lore (shop ID + the chest shop's live settings) and a clearer "owner + right-click to configure" line.
- **Re-clicking your own existing shop sign no longer errors** - it now reopens the config menu, so changing prices is a single click instead of sign demolition.
- The **SELL button in the shop menu shows the live total** you're about to receive, and sell stacks carry a price-hint lore line.

### 🏰 Guilds: ranks, mail & the score fix
- **PvE and PvP score now actually increase.** The score calls sat deep inside one giant entity-death handler, so any exception in boss/revenant/raid code above them silently skipped both (and duels skipped them entirely). Guild scoring is now isolated in its own guarded hooks and **duel kills count**.
- **Guild ranks added**: **Founder / Officer / Member** with a golden crown, name prefix, and roster display. Officers can invite; **only the founder promotes or demotes** via `/guild promote|demote <player>`.
- **Guild Mail added**: officers and the founder can send items + a note to members via `/guild mail <member> [subject]` (sends the item you're holding). Recipients claim attachments from the **Mail button in the guild menu** (or `/guild mail`) - attachments wait safely in the mailbox even offline, and mail expires after 7 days.
- **Stab Shot milestone is now recurring**: every **30 guild PvE score** a member personally earns grants *them* one Stab Shot straight to their inventory (or their guild mailbox if offline). The one-time PvP-30 founder reward is unchanged, and the guild skills menu shows live progress toward the next reward.

### 🪑 Chair seat fixed
- Sitting no longer floats you **on top of** the chair - the seat stand was spawning too high. You now sit properly *inside* the chair (and the old bogus height-limit popup that came with it is gone).

### 💰 Expeditions nerfed
- **Chests pay out exactly ONCE per run** - looting the same chest repeatedly (or re-entering and re-opening it) is dead; looted chests are renamed "Looted Chest" and remembered.
- **Chests are scarcer**: loot-chest rooms dropped from 25% to 5% of rooms (exactly one chest), treasure rooms 5% (one rich chest), cache/fountain rooms halved; the big-hall rich chest went from two to one.
- **Per-run chest cap: $25,000** from chests alone. Once hit, further chests pay nothing (ores and mob kills are uncapped), and the HUD still counts everything you've earned.
- **Void expedition chest payouts cut** (~100-2500 per chest, rich chests x2) - it used to print money.

### ⚔️ Enchanting fixes
- **Removed the offhand tome quick-fuse** ("hold a tome and right-click with the weapon in your other hand") - it hijacked right-clicks and could eat your weapon (one player lost a crossbow this way). Tome fusion now happens only in the **Item Forge** or on an **anvil**.
- **Revolver now truly supports Infinity**: with Infinity fused onto the revolver, the cylinder never consumes arrows - it synthesizes bolts when you're out, so all 6 shots fire every reload cycle.

### ⚠ Dangerous Mining Zones rework
- **Zones are now richer and worth the risk**: each zone is **seeded at generation** with bonus deepslate ore veins baked into its walls (coal through diamond/emerald, with a small **ancient debris** chance) plus a few soul torches so the pocket is visible. Up to **3 zones** now roll per start (was 2), with a fallback pool so a start without sealed pockets can still get a zone instead of silently spawning nothing (a warning is logged if all else fails).
- **Better payouts**: zone ore sells are up from 70% to a **55% double-drop** chance plus a **bigger cash bonus** (⅕ of the ore's value, was ⅐) and a small **mining XP kicker** on top.
- **Deadlier mobs**: cap raised to 8 hostiles (was 6), spawns 2-3 at a time, and the mix is nastier - **cave spiders and witches** join the roster, with a **30% chance of tier-2 variants** (was tier 1-2 at 50/50). Zone mobs that wander out are swept back so tiered mobs don't leak into normal caves.
- **Zone placement is smarter**: zones never spawn inside **land claims**, never overlap each other, and sit deeper (Y -8 to -32).

### 🚫 Anti-dupe: zones only pay on natural ore
- **Placed blocks no longer pay the dangerous-zone bonus.** Every player-placed block is tracked (creative included), so the old exploit of **placing diamond/emerald ore inside a zone and re-mining it** for infinite bonus cash and drops is gone. Placed ore inside a zone breaks normally - it just pays nothing extra.
- The bonus now **only applies to ore blocks** (all overworld + nether ores and ancient debris). Stone, deepslate and everything else mined inside a zone pays nothing extra, so the zone is a real gamble on real ore.
- Creative mode never pays the bonus (matching Mining Pity's rule).

### 📉 Skills are harder to level
- **XP curve is steeper**: each level now needs `25 + 45×level` XP (was `20 + 40×level`) - early levels are only slightly slower, but **levels 8-10 take noticeably longer** (total to 10: ~2,275 XP vs ~1,820, +25%).
- **The permanent ×1.5 XP bonus is gone.** It's now a **fast-start bonus that fades out**: ×1.5 at skill level 0, shrinking to ×1.0 by level 4.
- **XP-boost upgrades were nerfed across the board**: Prospector, Sow & Reap, Timber, Schmoozer and Arcane Focus now give **+15/30/45% XP** (was +25/50/75%, or +20/40/60% for Schmoozer).

## 1.0.45

### 🪑 Chairs
- **Chairs are no longer craftable** - the 2-plank recipe is gone. Chairs are still obtainable from the **shop** (Exclusive, $150) and `/ff give chair`.

### 🐛 Ascended Wither fixes (bug & performance audit)
- **King promotion can no longer be used as a free mid-fight heal** - the right-click window was inverted, accepting offers *after* the fight started (skull consumed, full heal, ×1.5 HP) and rejecting them during the actual summoning show. It now only works while the summon animation is running, as intended.
- **Re-adopted withers are no longer auto-promoted to KINGs**: a wither whose chunk unloaded and re-adopted on return was detected by max-HP (>300), which every Ascended Wither satisfies - silently making every resumed fight a King with a full heal. Detection is now by custom name; re-adopted fights keep their current health instead of healing to full.
- **Domain soul-sand restore fixed**: the arena ring was placed one block above where restore looked, so the carpet stayed forever (and could smother builds). Placed/removed positions now match exactly, and the ring uses soul soil (no mob-spawning) while player soul sand inside the ring is never eaten.
- **Death sequence is now fully invulnerable**: /kill or void damage during the 7s death animation bypassed the gate and fired vanilla death on top of it (double nether star + loot).
- **Anti-burrow rebuild restores block entities** - carved chests/furnaces/spawners come back with their contents instead of as empty husks; rebuilds survive chunk unload/reload instead of being silently dropped; blocks built into the hole meanwhile are respected; and the claim check now happens before capture.
- **Orphaned Wither Knights are swept** - a crash mid-fight left named, persistent, armoured knights standing around forever; they're tagged and cleaned up like the animation displays.
- **Underground fights spawn knights/mist at the arena level** instead of on the (possibly 40+ blocks away) surface heightmap.
- **Performance**: wither-mist pools and homing skulls tick once per server tick instead of once per concurrent fight (double-ticking doubled wither stacks and particles with two fights live); per-ability log spam demoted to debug; early-exit also covers pools/seekers/transforms; display spawn/death animations actually animate now (final transform is applied on a later tick instead of overwriting the start transform same-tick).

### 📚 Loot balance
- **Custom enchant tomes no longer spawn in generated chests** - every randomly generated chest (dungeons, mineshafts, temples, strongholds, everything) used to roll a hidden tome pool, flooding exploration with enchant books. Tomes are now exclusive to their intended sources: **librarians**, boss drops, the forge, loot boxes and `/ff give`.

## 1.0.44

### 💀 The Ascended Wither (reworkable Wither boss fight)
- **New Wither boss fight, toggle in `/ff config` ("Reworked Wither", **default ON**) - every summoned Wither is now the **Ascended Wither**: 600 HP, higher armour, and a move set instead of fly-around-and-shoot. Admins who prefer the vanilla fight flip it off in the admin menu; the toggle persists in config.json and reverts future withers instantly.
- **Moves (phase 1)**:
  - **Bedrock Charge** - locks on and plows straight through terrain (vanilla block destruction rules + claim protection still apply).
  - **Burst** - nova knockback to escape melee combos.
  - **Skull volleys** with occasional **homing seeker skulls** that steer after you - keep moving.
  - **Anti-burrow skulls** - a piercing blue skull shreds up to 4 layers of soft *or hard* cover (dirt, stone, obsidian) toward you, punishing cheap bunkers. Every carved block is **restored ~6s later**, so the arena heals itself.
  - **Wither Knights** - wither-skeleton waves summoned by phase 2 (4 per wave for the King, named/armoured).
  - **Active side heads** - the left/right heads independently spit lingering **wither-mist pools** that wither anyone standing in them.
- **Phase 2 (below 50%)**: the Wither **ascends** (temporarily immune to projectiles), then **slams down** onto its target's position - shockwave knockback, wither, and a shallow crater (rebuilt by Explosion Rebuild if on). Knights waves start here.
- **The parry (30% HP)**: once per fight the Wither raises a **skull shield** that reflects arrows and bolts back at their shooters for 10 seconds - switch to blades.
- **Domain Expansion** (phase 2, occasionally): a soul-sand sanctum. Everyone inside is **withered for real** - the domain's wither bypasses the **wither skeleton crown** (crown regen-strip is suspended inside the sanctum, so even crown wearers wither) - plus the boss's own dark damage, a soul-sand ground ring, and **any withered player damaged near the Wither feeds it** (2× for the King - don't stay withered!).
- **The King fight**: while a summoning Ascended Wither is rising (**~13s window - watch for the hint**), **right-click it holding a Wither Skeleton Skull** to offer it - a **KING** takes over: ×1.5 HP, faster, harder-hitting, bigger skull fans, Royal Wither Knight waves, and it **siphons healing from players still suffering wither** near it (don't stay withered!). Balanced for survival: no potion/food drain, no gear destruction, and its death drops extra wither skeleton skulls + **Wither Essence** alongside the nether star.
- **Cinematics**: a longer **summon animation** (soul-fire spiral, rising soul-sand pillars, shrinking cocoon shell, title card), a custom **death sequence** (soul pillars, a much **bigger explosion**, then a rising nether-star **item-display ceremony** with a light beam before the real star drops).
- **Wither Skeleton Skulls can no longer be used as beacon payment** (they're the King's offering now).
- Toggle lives in `/ff config` (the admin menu) and persists in config.json; disabling reverts future withers to vanilla instantly. Crash-safe: animation displays are tagged and swept if the server dies mid-show.

## 1.0.43

### ⚡ Server performance boost (tick loop overhaul)
- **/ff perf (OP)**: a live profiler for the mod itself. Shows the server's MSPT/TPS plus every Fortune & Favors tick system sorted by how much of the tick budget it eats (avg + worst 5s spike, color-coded). `/ff perf off` stops it entirely (zero overhead) - so "the server feels laggy" now has a first answer instead of guesswork. The profiler rides the mod's existing safety wrapper, so nothing else had to change to get per-system numbers.
- **Per-player tick handlers are gated on the gear that needs them.** The possessed-mask, mindbinder-shroud and sculk-sense ticks (each a nearby-entity scan) now run only for players actually wearing that piece; the death/bounty compass ticks only for players actually holding one. On a normal SMP (nobody in boss gear) this removes up to **5 entity scans × 20/s × every online player** that previously ran unconditionally.
- **Legacy weapon reapply is gated on material** (sword/axe tag) before touching the item's custom data - previously every player's held item had its whole NBT tag copied 20×/s to check for a Legacy enchant that was almost never there.
- **Wall-clock schedulers poll once a second instead of every tick**: contracts, player raids, lottery draw check, bank interest, the newspaper and the weekly leaderboard rollover all did date/time parsing 20×/s for events that fire at most once per day/week. The lottery, newspaper and challenges were additionally doing a full string rebuild per tick; now one `System.currentTimeMillis()` compare.
- **Boss entity lookup no longer scans every dimension every tick.** Each active boss remembers its level (self-populating cache with a full-scan fallback), and the boss-bar player-add scan runs 1×/s instead of 20×.
- **Tab-list dimension-change detection is allocation-free**: identity-compares the dimension key instead of building and comparing two strings per player per tick.
- **Custom-enchant checks got a fast negative path**: `has()` for the many level-1 enchants answers from a containment check, and the Legacy weapon test checks the material tag before reading any custom data. These run inside every combat, projectile and per-player tick path.
- Everything is behavior-preserving: same effects, same cadence for anything a player can notice, same fail-safe wrappers. Run `/ff perf` before and after on a busy server to see the difference (the mod's own share of the tick typically drops to well under a millisecond when nobody is wearing special gear).

## 1.0.42

### 🧹 Floating popup leak fixed (the "Sticky!" stacking bug)
- **The bug**: proc popups ("§aSticky!", "§7Seismic!", "§c♥ +4", ...), possessed-mask price labels ("§a$12"), and grave name labels are real floating-text entities. They were spawned **without any marker**, so when the server crashed or was force-quit (before their 1.5s expiry sweep ran) they were saved into the world — and on reload nothing owned them anymore. Every crash stacked a few more, forever. On an SMP with fights and mask users this piles up into thousands of ticking text displays and visible server lag.
- **Every popup is now tagged at spawn** (`ff_display` + `ff_display_tmp` for ephemeral ones; grave labels get `ff_display` only) — no TextDisplay the mod spawns is anonymous anymore.
- **A stale-display sweep runs at startup and every 5 minutes**, discarding:
  - tmp-tagged popups that the live systems no longer track (crash leftovers, and mask labels whose wearer logged out mid-glow — a second leak found while fixing this one);
  - tagged grave labels whose player-head skull is gone (e.g. removed with worldedit);
  - **untagged strays from older builds**, recognized by their exact old popup texts + the old spawn pattern, so your current world's existing pile gets cleaned without touching admin decor or vanilla displays.
- Live popups and mask labels are tracked in memory, so the sweep can never blink an actively-shown popup; if a mask-wearer returns to a mob whose orphaned label was swept, the mask-sense system now **adopts the surviving label** instead of stacking a duplicate.
- Sweep results are logged (`Discarded N stale floating-text display(s)`), so you can see how many were eating your TPS.

## 1.0.41

### 🗡 Lunge is now the vanilla spear + vanilla lunge enchant
- The Lunge Spear is now exactly what it was always supposed to be: a **vanilla `minecraft:[material]_spear`** (wooden → netherite) enchanted with the **vanilla `minecraft:lunge`** enchantment. No trident, no custom tome enchant.
- The mod's signature move is unchanged and now keys off the real thing: **right-click a vanilla spear with `minecraft:lunge` on it while wearing an elytra** to dash in your look direction (2.5s cooldown, spear SFX + spark ring / cloud trail / end-rod streak / sonic boom / gust VFX, Sky Spears + Spear Rampage advancements).
- **Any spear works** — wooden, stone, copper, iron, golden, diamond, netherite — as long as it carries `minecraft:lunge` (enchanting table, anvil, or loot; 26.2 ships the enchantment and the `#minecraft:enchantable/lunge` → `#minecraft:spears` tag natively).
- **The custom `ff_lunge` tome enchant is removed**: no more Lunge tomes from bosses, loot chests, librarian trades, or the recipe/creative menus.
- **`/ff give lunge` / `lungespear` / `spear`** now hands out a **netherite spear with Lunge III** (vanilla item, vanilla enchantment tooltip).
- Existing old tridents/tomes with the custom `ff_lunge` tag go quiet — they no longer trigger the dash; craft/enchant a real spear instead.

## 1.0.40

### 🏷 Tags & Titles fixes
- **New "Show Tags & Titles" toggle in the /tags menu** (row 1, next to the position setting): flip it and your tag and title disappear everywhere - chat and the tab list - for everyone. Per-player, saved per player, and the tab list refreshes instantly.
- **The server-wide Tags feature toggle actually turns tags off now**: it used to leave stored/custom tags visible (only titles and the Owner fallback hid), so "tags off" looked half-broken. Now everything cosmetic disappears when it's off, and the change applies to the tab list immediately. Only the bounty-system ☠ WANTED marker stays - that's gameplay info, not cosmetics.
- **Creator title/tag overlap fixed**: if the mod creator has an older title active, it no longer renders next to the automatic "Creator of Mod" tag - the tag is the canonical one and any old title is suppressed, so you can't get `[Old Title] Poorboy7890 [Creator of Mod]` anymore.

## 1.0.39

### 🛋 /ff afk
- **`/ff afk`** marks you away - the tab list shows a gray **`[AFK]`** marker on your name (next to your title/tag/dimension icons). Run it again to clear.
- **Auto-trigger**: stand completely still (no movement, no turning) for **5 minutes** and you're marked AFK automatically.
- **Auto-clear**: move, turn, or type a chat message and the marker vanishes instantly (and the tab list refreshes right away).
- Fully server-side, session-only - logging out clears it, and vanilla clients see the marker with no extra mods.

## 1.0.38

### 💥 Sonic Boom (elytra speed)
- **Port of the Sonic Boom mod you played with** (it was built for 1.21.8; it's now part of Fortune & Favors on 26.2, fully server-side so vanilla clients see and hear everything).
- Glide at **~1.7+ blocks/tick with an elytra** for just over a second and you break the sound barrier: a **thunderclap heard for blocks** (warden boom sound), a **shockwave ring** + spark ring + air burst, and a **one-second forward launch** in your look direction.
- The boom then needs **60 seconds to recharge**; the first one ever awards the **Sonic BOOOOM!** advancement (once per player).

## 1.0.37

### 🧭 Waypoints save the moment you set them
- Waypoints (and last-death records) were only written to the world file on the 2-minute autosave sweep, so a crash or quick quit could lose them. Every set / remove / clear now writes **immediately** to the world's `wormhole_waypoints.json` - they live in the world's own data folder, so they are per-world and survive restarts.

### 🎒 Backpack features are now forged in, not built in
- **Nothing comes with a fresh backpack anymore.** The Workbench, Ender Chest and Jukebox toolbar buttons are only present after the matching Item Forge fusion, and unfused packs show a grayed "Not installed" placeholder telling you what to fuse:
  - Backpack + **Crafting Table** → Workbench button
  - Backpack + **Ender Pouch** → Ender Chest button
  - Backpack + **Jukebox** → Jukebox button (new fusion - the Item Forge routes jukeboxes into the material slot)

### 🎧 Jukebox stereo fix
- Your own disc music used to play hard-panned to one side (the sound's position was being fed world coordinates while flagged listener-relative). It now plays dead center and follows you by definition. Nearby players' boombox playback is unchanged.

### 🎒 The backpack is now a real base-on-the-go
- **Workbench must be built in**: the Workbench button no longer works on every backpack. Fuse a **Crafting Table** into the backpack in the **Item Forge** (backpack + table, table is consumed) and the pack permanently gains a built-in Workbench - its lore says so too.
- **Workbench actually opens the crafting GUI**: the old button silently failed because the vanilla crafting menu closes itself unless it was opened next to a real crafting table. The backpack's crafting menu now works anywhere - a full 3x3, no table needed.
- **Jukebox! MUSIC on the go**: the "??? Coming soon" placeholder is now a **Jukebox**. Click it with a music disc on your cursor - the disc disappears into the pack and the song starts playing, **following you** (full volume wherever you walk). It **never breaks or restarts** - when the song ends it seamlessly loops again. Click the jukebox empty-handed to **get the disc back** and stop the music. The disc is stored inside the backpack, so it survives relogs (music resumes when you reopen the pack), and closing the backpack never stops the show. Works with every vanilla + datapack disc; needs the mod's client for sound (vanilla clients just see the jukebox).
- **Boombox broadcast**: the jukebox is a real speaker now - players who come within ~32 blocks of the owner hear the same song as a positional sound that tracks the owner with linear volume falloff. Walk out of range and it fades away (with a little hysteresis so the edge doesn't flicker); walk back in and the song picks up. Every boombox in range plays at once, and ejecting/stopping the disc silences the whole audience.
- **Toolbar shift-click fix**: toolbar buttons (and the loaded disc) can no longer be shift-clicked out of the menu, closing a small dupe hole.

### 🔧 26.2 launch crash fixed
- The 1.0.36 build **crashed on startup** on 26.2: the Moonwalk enchant's mixin still injected into `getEffectiveGravity` with the old `(LivingEntity, ...)` handler shape, which killed the whole mod during mixin application ("Invalid descriptor ... Expected (CallbackInfoReturnable)V"). The handler now matches 26.2's no-arg method. Verified: dev server and client both boot with zero mixin errors.

### 📜 /ff give menu catch-up
- The item catalog (and `/ff give`) now includes everything that had fallen behind: **Repairing Membrane** (`/ff give membrane`), all four **Mystery Keys** (`commonkey`/`rarekey`/`epickey`/`legendarykey`) and the **Golden Apple Head** (`goldenapplehead`). The **Death Compass** (`deathcompass`) and **Bounty Compass** (`bountycompass`) were verified present.

## 1.0.36

### ⚔️ Legacy, runes, restore & lunge fixes
- **Legacy bows nerfed**: full-power on release is gone - bows now charge 10% faster instead, so the draw animation plays properly and tap-spam is no longer instant max damage. The -30% damage tradeoff stays on swords and axes.
- **Legacy works on fishing rods now**: rods were excluded from the enchant's applicable items, so the tome could never be applied. Fixed - the rod's faster bobber + knockback effects now actually trigger.
- **Lunge spear removed**: there is no special crafted spear anymore - it's a normal trident with the **LUNGE** enchant (that was always what triggered it). The recipe, give alias and references are gone; `/ff give lunge` now hands out the LUNGE tome.
- **Moonwalk fixed**: the old velocity nudge was being overwritten every tick by vanilla elytra physics. Gravity is now reduced directly while fall-flying with MOONWALK on the chestplate (25% per level), so the glide actually floats.
- **Runes work in the Item Forge**: runes were falling through the slot router into the wrong slot and never reached the material slot, so fusion silently failed. They now route to the material slot and fuse exactly like the rune menu does (weapon runes only on weapons, armor runes only on armor).
- **/ff restore no longer wipes inventories**: it used to clear the player's whole inventory first, then refill from a possibly stale snapshot - which nuked items picked up since the last snapshot and everything graves had just returned. Restore is now additive: snapshot items merge back into free slots or stack onto matching stacks, and nothing currently held is ever removed.

## 1.0.35

### ⛏️ Mining pity: creative-proof + cleaner VFX
- **Creative mode no longer triggers pity drops** - survival/adventure players only, so creative is no longer a free resource faucet.
- **Better, lighter VFX**: the pity pop is now a compact puff of dust in the dropped item's own color (iron gray, diamond cyan, emerald green, etc.) + a few gold sparks + a soft chime, replacing the three overlapping particle showers.
- **Bug fixes**: placed-block tracking is now keyed by dimension + position, so a block you placed in the Overworld can no longer suppress a pity drop for a natural block at the same coordinates in the Nether/End. The placed-block map also self-prunes during heavy build sessions so it stays bounded.

## 1.0.34

### 🔮 Four new runes + armor rune sockets
- **Rune of Reach** (+1 block melee reach while held) and **Rune of Lifesteal** (heal 1 heart on every hit) join the weapon lineup — seven weapon runes in total.
- **Rune of Warding** (+3 armor, +1 toughness) and **Rune of Fortitude** (+2 max hearts) are the first **armor runes** — they socket into helmets, chestplates, leggings and boots and apply while worn.
- The **Rune menu** grew to a 9x5 layout with a weapon-rune row and an armor-rune row; socketing, the right-click fuse, the Item Forge and the new **Bastion** achievement all support armor pieces.
- New runes drop from bosses (Warden can drop Reach/Warding/Fortitude, Mindbinder can drop Lifesteal), Mystery Chests and raids; all four are in `/ff give` and the give catalog.
- `all_runes` (now "all seven weapon runes") description updated; new **all_armor_runes** advancement.

## 1.0.33

### ⚔️ Duels: replay-mod friendly + bot fixes
- The duel arena now **pre-generates every chunk** in the arena bounds before the fight starts, so the duel dimension fully exists server-side from second zero — replay/recording mods (ReplayMod, Flashback) no longer see a hollow, unloaded dimension.
- **Bots take bow damage now.** Bot damage is intercepted before the vanilla invulnerability-frame gate, so arrows and other projectiles actually land instead of being swallowed by the melee-spam hurt cooldown.
- **Bots stop cheating:** their melee is throttled by a per-bot tick window (no more machine-gun hits), and their strafe speed is capped to sane values.

### 🏆 Custom enchant tomes in the wild
- Every custom enchant tome can now be found in **randomly generated loot chests** (dungeons, temples, mineshafts…) — rarer tomes are weighted lower, Legacy is extra rare.
- **Librarians (level 3+) sell random tomes** for emeralds on restock — one tome offer per restock, priced 24–40 emeralds.
- Tome levels scale up to each enchant's max (Legacy is always level I).

### 🎮 /ff is now an OP command hub
- Bare `/ff` opens the **FF Admin Hub**: a GUI with every admin subcommand — Give Items, Config, Events, Restore, Gems, Vanish, Backup, Title, test bosses, Compass FX toggle, Claim Soulbound, Leave Realm, Help and Version. Each button runs the exact same command as typing it. Non-OPs get pointed to /menu instead.

### 🕹 Other fixes & additions
- **Playtime stats**: the Streak menu now shows your **total time on the server** and **first-join date** (tracked server-side, survives crashes/restarts).
- **Bounty Compass** (craft: compass + redstone ring, or `/ff give bountycompass`) — a flame-thread points at the **highest-bounty player within 20,000 blocks** and right-click announces their exact position.
- **Sculk Fruit** from the Elder Warden (also `/ff give sculkfood`) — restores hunger, 10s of sculk sight + regeneration.
- **Legacy enchantment** is complete: swords/axes swing instantly but deal **30% less damage**, bows fire full-power instantly (spam-friendly), fishing rods cast a faster bobber that **knocks back** on hit.
- **Death Compass FX toggle**: `/ff compassfx` (or the Admin Hub button) silences the grave-pulse tracking beam/beacon per player.
- **Random teleport now reaches 1,000 blocks** from the wormhole potion.
- **Tab-list name color fixes**: prefixes/colors now refresh on death and on dimension change, so your color never sticks after you leave a realm.
- **Raid spawns polished**: raiders spawn on the surface near the player with a safe spawn-height search (solid ground + 2 air blocks), never stuck underground or inside blocks.
- **Recipe fixed**: the Bounty Compass craft matches its lore.

## 1.0.32

### ☠ Death Compass
- **A compass that remembers where you died** — craft it (compass + 4 soul sand in a cross), buy it from the **Gem Shop** (6 gems), or `/ff give deathcompass`.
- **Hold it to track your grave**: a soul-flame thread reaches from your hand toward your last death location (through walls), and your action bar shows the distance + direction (e.g. "☠ Grave 132m NW"). Works across dimensions — it tells you which world the grave is in.
- **Right-click for a sonar ping**: a soul-fire burst + end-rod beacon column marks the grave so you can see it across the map, the exact coords are announced in chat, and a bell + respawn-anchor sound plays. 5s ping cooldown.
- **It goes quiet once you retrieve your grave** — claiming your grave clears the record. Death locations now **survive server restarts** too (saved alongside the wormhole waypoints), so the compass still works after a restart.
- **Grave Hunter** advancement for your first ping.
- 100% server-side: the tracking, particles and sounds all emit from the server and auto-sync to every client.

### 🎒 Backpack workbench row
- Every backpack now has a **toolbar row** under its storage: a **Workbench** button (portable 3×3 crafting table — works anywhere, no table needed) and an **Ender Chest** button (opens your shared ender chest wherever you are).
- A **"??? Coming soon"** placeholder sits next to them — more backpack features are on the way.
- Tiers 1–4 gained a full extra row (storage unchanged); the max-tier 6-row pack keeps its size and hosts the buttons at the end of its bottom row.

## 1.0.31

### 🗡 Lunge Spear is a normal spear with the Lunge enchantment
- The Lunge Spear is no longer a special tagged item — it's a **regular trident enchanted with the mod's new Lunge enchantment** (`Lunge I`, shown in its lore like every other custom enchant).
- The enchantment is what makes the lunge work: hold any **trident with Lunge** and **right-click** while wearing an elytra to dash in your look direction (2.5s cooldown, works gliding or on the ground). It still plays the **spear lunge SFX** (riptide whoosh + trident throw) and the spear VFX (spark shockwave ring, cloud trail, end-rod streak, sonic boom tip, gust burst).
- The recipe now crafts a normal trident carrying Lunge I (phantom membrane + trident + phantom membrane), `/ff give lungespear` hands out the same item, and the enchantment is fully server-side — the server's use-item hook owns the move, so vanilla clients behave identically.

## 1.0.30

### 🪑 Chairs fixed + the Chair Menu
- **Sitting works again** (and no more bogus "height limit" popup): the seat is no longer a zero-size marker stand (the classic position glitch), it's a small invisible stand with its Y clamped below the build limit — sitting near the top of the world can't trigger the build-height message anymore.
- **Shift-right-click works again** — the old use-block hook returned early on sneak-clicks, so the pickup branch was never reached. Sneak-right-clicking a chair now opens the new **Chair Menu**.
- **Chair Menu** (owner-only): shows the owner card (who owns it + exact position), a **Pick Up** button that returns the chair as an item (ejecting anyone sitting on it first), and **9 wood variants** — click Oak/Spruce/Birch/Jungle/Acacia/Dark Oak/Mangrove/Cherry/**Pale Oak** to restyle the placed chair in place, keeping its facing. The current style is highlighted with a glint.
- The block still can't be mined — pickup is the only way to move it, so the item can never be lost.

### 🌪 Wormhole combat lock
- Taking any damage **combat-tags you for 3 seconds** — while tagged, every wormhole action (opening the window, warping to waypoints/respawn/death/random, requesting a player warp, even setting waypoints) is refused.
- **Fully silent as requested**: no chat spam, no timers while you wait. The only feedback is when you *try* to warp while tagged — the wormhole window closes and one action-bar line tells you how long is left (e.g. "⚔ Combat tag - wormhole locked for 2.4s").

### 🔮 Runes fixed and honest
- **Fixed "using one rune blocks the rest"**: socketing (Rune Menu, Item Forge, and the two-hand quick-fuse) now consumes exactly one rune — the old path double-consumed a stack and could leave the menu in a state where nothing else could be applied.
- **Rune fusion in the Item Forge**: put any sword/axe in the forge with a rune in the material slot to fuse it (same "one of each rune per weapon" rule, duplicates refused).
- **Descriptions now match reality**: the Haste rune really adds **+8% attack speed** (the lore claimed 5%), and every rune card spells out the "one of each rune fits per weapon, they all stack" rule so the system is finally self-explanatory.

### 🗡 Lunge Spear rework
- **You lunge with the spear, not your jump** — the double-jump trigger is completely removed. Hold the new **Lunge Spear** (trident-style item, craftable: phantom membrane + echo shard + stick) and **right-click** while wearing an elytra to dash in your look direction (works on ground and while gliding, 2.5s cooldown).
- **Spear sound**: riptide whoosh + trident throw instead of the elytra glide noise.
- **Spear VFX**: electric-spark shockwave ring, cloud trail behind the dash, end-rod streak along your aim, sonic boom at the tip and a gust burst.
- Get it from `/ff give lungespear`, the recipe, or the Recipe menu.

### 🏆 12 new advancements
- **Take a Seat** (sit on a chair), **Interior Designer** (restyle a chair into another wood), **Socket Sorcery** (fuse your first rune), **Full Rune Set** (all five runes on one weapon), **Sky Spears** (first spear lunge), **Spear Rampage** (lunge straight up), **Lucky Streak** (win the weekly lottery), **Bank Heist Insurance** (upgrade your bank), **Vault Tycoon** (max bank level 5), **Wayfarer** (set all 3 wormhole waypoints), **Key Master** (open a Mystery Chest), **Duel Champion** (win a PvP duel).

### 📰 Smarter newspaper
- **Weekly lottery sales**: when the pot is drawn, the paper reports how many tickets were sold for the pot and who made the **biggest buy-in**.
- **Bank interest day**: the paper reports the **biggest interest payout** of the day (amount + who + their rate).
- **More real interactions reported**: duel wins (mode + winner), guild **mine upgrades** (guild, level, cost), **bank upgrades** (player, level), alongside the existing boss kills, bounties, and auction stories.

## 1.0.29

### 🏦 Bank rework — one upgrade, five clear levels
- **The whole bank is one upgrade now** (levels 1–5): each level raises the money vault, the XP vault, AND daily interest together — no more three separate upgrade tracks.
- **Level 1 stores way less** ($50k cash / ~level 16 XP, down from $250k / ~level 30) and **upgrades cost far less**: $10k → $50k → $250k → $1M (was $25k → $2.5M across three tracks).
- **Interest gets *better* with every level**: 0.5% → 0.75% → 1% → 1.5% → 2% per day, with the daily cap rising $500 → $200k. Still capped, so even maxed vaults grow slowly.
- **UI rebuilt from scratch** — one tidy card layout: money row (deposit/withdraw half or all + meter), XP row (10 levels/all + meter), and a single **Upgrade Bank** button with the full level table on the help item. Old bank accounts and upgrades carry over (best tier kept).

### 🛒 Gem Shop fixes
- **Fixed the Mystery Key**: buying one now grants a *real* Mystery Key (opens the Mystery Chest menu) instead of a plain tripwire hook that did nothing.
- **Fixed Tag Recolor**: it now actually resets your tag color to the default green (it used to hand you a plain name tag).

### 🎰 Lottery seed pot
- The weekly lottery now **starts with a $50,000 pot** (server-funded), and every new week's pot is topped back up to that seed — jackpots are never empty again.

### ⛏️ Guild mine rebalance
- Mine upgrade costs slashed: **$25k → $75k → $250k → $750k → $2.5M → $10M** (was $100k → $250M). The expensive tiers are actually reachable now.

### 🎒 Shop cleanup
- **Removed the Bundle from the Exclusive shop** — it was a reskinned backpack. It's still craftable (2 planks) and upgradeable at the Item Forge.

## 1.0.28

### 🛠 Crash fix: Cloth Config is now truly optional
- **Fixed a startup crash** (`NoClassDefFoundError: me/shedaniel/autoconfig/ConfigData`) on installs without Cloth Config — the mod jar only *suggests* cloth-config, but the client entrypoint loaded its classes unconditionally, so any instance (like a minimal test profile) without it crashed on launch.
- All config values now live in a plain state class that never touches Cloth; the config file (`config/fortuneandfavors.json`) is loaded/saved by the mod itself in the exact same format, so settings survive with or without Cloth installed.
- **With Cloth Config present nothing changes**: ModMenu still gets the full Cloth screen, server settings still push to the server (ops only), and the OP join auto-push still fires once per join.
- **Without Cloth Config**: no crash, client FX toggles still work from the shared file, and ModMenu simply shows no config button.

## 1.0.27

### 🔒 Config security (Cloth Config / ModMenu)
- **Server settings can now only be changed by ops on that server.** Saving the config screen on a server where you don't have OP no longer touches the server at all — previously a save *could* send a config packet the server would apply, and a non-admin save also triggered a useless "not applied" request round-trip.
- **OP auto-push**: if you're an op, your locally saved server settings (NKI, features, timers…) are **automatically applied when you join your server** — change NKI on in Cloth Config, join with OP, and it's on. Regular players joining never push anything.
- Two ops can't bounce configs at each other: the auto-push fires only once, on join, and the server broadcast afterwards never re-pushes.
- Client FX toggles (overlays, flashes, poses) keep working for everyone — those were always local-only.

### ✨ Waypoint setting VFX
- Setting a wormhole waypoint now plays a **purple portal-dust ring** that spirals outward around you, with **reverse-portal sparkles** climbing above it and a **respawn-anchor charge sound** — matching the existing warp effects.

## 1.0.26

### 🏦 The Bank (`/bank`, `/menu → Bank`)
- **Two safe vaults**: a **money vault** and an **XP vault**, both stored server-side so **death and raids can never touch them**.
- **Meters everywhere**: both vaults show a live progress bar — deposits stop exactly at capacity, so you can never overfill.
- **Interest that can't snowball**: banked cash earns **daily interest** (~every 20 real hours) at a small rate that **tapers as your vault fills** — a $1M vault earns roughly $150/day no matter what, so 1M can never become 1B. The payout is also capped by your **interest cap**.
- **Upgrades**: **vault capacity** ($25k → $100M in 5 tiers, paid in cash), **interest cap** ($1k → $15k/day, paid in cash) and **XP capacity** (≈ level 30 → level 120, paid in XP levels).
- **Lossless XP**: the vault stores raw XP points using vanilla's exact level curve — deposit 5 levels, get 5 levels back. Deposit/withdraw by 5 levels or everything, on both vaults.
- Interest is paid even while you're offline; the window explains every rule inline.

### 🪑 The Chair
- A **simple, cheap furniture block**: craft it from **2 oak planks** (vertical), buy it in the shop's Exclusive category ($150), or `/ff give chair`.
- **Right-click to sit** — you actually sit on the seat, facing away from the chair's back. Sneak to get up.
- **Sneak-right-click to pick it up** — you always get the exact Chair item back. The block **can't be mined**, so the server-side item can never be lost to a bad break; the pickup is the only way to move it.
- Only the owner can pick it up. Chairs survive server restarts (they're tracked like every other machine), anyone who relogs while seated is re-seated, and anyone sitting on a chair is politely ejected when it's picked up.

### 👋 A warmer welcome
- The **first-join welcome menu** is refreshed: the intro now mentions the bank and XP vaults, includes a tip about the new Chair, and gained a direct **"Open Your Bank"** button so new players can protect their first earnings immediately.

### 📚 Also
- `/bank` opens the bank directly; the bank and chair are in `/ff help` (new `bank` topic) and the `/menu` hub gained a Bank button.
- The chair is in the **Recipe menu** (`/menu → Recipes`).

## 1.0.25

### ⚙️ One config: Cloth Config only
- **OneConfig removed** — Fortune & Favors now has a single config screen built on the **Cloth Config API** (ModMenu → Fortune & Favors, or the `client`/`server` categories). The bundled OneConfig screen was overriding Cloth's in ModMenu and the two configs fought over values; OneConfig is gone entirely (dependency, screen, and sync code stripped) so what you see is what applies.
- **Raid Cooldown toggle added** — the last server option missing from the config screen (`raid_cooldown`) is now exposed alongside auction minutes, boss minions, backups and the rest, and round-trips through the server-sync payload.

### 🎟 Weekly Lottery (`/lottery`)
- **A server-wide jackpot**: buy tickets for **$100 each** (`/lottery` GUI, `/lottery buy <n>`, or the new button in `/menu`), max 100 per player per week.
- **Draws every Monday at midnight EST** — one weighted winner takes **75% of the pot**, **25% rolls over** so the jackpot keeps growing; if nobody buys in, the whole pot carries to next week.
- **Reported by the newspaper**: the draw is announced in chat and in the paper's new **LOTTERY** section (winner + jackpot), with the current pot shown on every other edition. Payouts go straight to the winner's balance, so it works even if they're offline.
- Tickets, the pot and the draw state persist in `lottery.json` with the mod's normal autosave/backup handling.

### 🥷 Admin vanish (`/ff vanish`)
- **Stealth toggle** (OP): while vanished you are **removed from everyone's tab list**, become **invisible** in the world, and everyone sees you **leave the game**; run it again to reappear (they see you join). **Chat is muted while hidden** so you never blow cover. Perfect for scouting, staff supervision, or a quiet prank.
- **Invisible to every targeting/tracking system** — not just the tab. A vanished player is excluded from `@a`/`@p`/`@s`/name selectors, `/tp` and `/execute`, every `EntityArgument.player()`-based command (`/duel`, `/balance <p>`, `/bounty`, `/trade`, `/guild invite`, boss/raid target resolution), the `/list`/online-name list, and the tab list — so nobody can look you up, teleport to you, or challenge you while hidden. Your own commands still work (self is always selectable), and all server housekeeping loops still reach you so effects, particles and saving keep running.
- **Fully safe while hidden**: a vanished player takes **zero damage** (player attacks, mobs, fall, fire, void, TNT), and **mobs never pick them as a target**. So vanish is genuinely invisible *and* invulnerable.
- **Armor (and its tell) removed**: while hidden your **armor is unequipped and stashed** so the ghost armor layer can't flicker around you and give your position away. It's kept safe on your own player — everything re-equips the instant you unvanish (or drop the game), so nothing is ever lost.


## 1.0.24

### ❄️ Ice Queen's realm can open again
- **"The Snow Queen / Ice Queen's realm refused to open" fixed** — the `fortuneandfavors:snow_queen_realm` dimension was referenced in code but its `dimension` + `dimension_type` files were never bundled, so `server.getLevel(SNOW_REALM)` returned null and the portal silently cancelled. The realm's `dimension/snow_queen_realm.json` (void/flat generator, snowy biome) and `dimension_type/snow_queen_realm.json` (bright overworld-style sky, skylight, no ceiling) are now bundled, mirroring how the duel arena was fixed.

### 📚 Creative tomes: every level, no fake ones
- The creative menus (Fortune & Favors tab + Combat tab) now carry **every real level** of each custom enchant: the original five tomes at **I–V**, Sharpshooter / Recoil / Hunter's Mark / Heavy Bolt / Fragility / Combo at **I–III**, and single-level enchants (**Chainfire, Sniper, Revolver, Berserker**) at **I only** — no more meaningless Sniper II/III or Revolver II/III (their effects don't scale, so those levels were removed everywhere, including `/ff give`).
- **Bolt Bringer is finally in the menus** — it's a real (registry) enchantment from the `boltbringer.json` datapack, so it never showed up with the NBT-based tomes. It's now in both creative tabs and `/ff give` (`boltbringer`, `boltbringer1-3`) as genuine enchanted books **I–III**. Because it's a real enchantment, it already works in anvils and the enchanting table natively.

### ⚒ Custom enchants now work in anvils
- Put any custom tome (original five or CC combat enchants) in an anvil with a sword/axe/mace/trident/bow/crossbow (either slot order) and it fuses like a vanilla enchanted book — small level cost (1–3), tome consumed on take, rename still applies. The pointless "custom tome + vanilla enchanted book" merge is blocked so the tome isn't eaten for nothing.

### 🧿 Sculk Medallion is craftable at last
- The Elder Warden's **Sculk Infused Medallion** now has its real crafting recipe (it was help-text-only, like the boss tokens): 4 echo shards + 4 gold blocks around a sculk sensor. Crafted medallions are byte-identical to `/ff give sculk_medallion` and summon the Elder Warden normally.

### 🎒 Backpack recipe verified
- Confirmed the backpack recipe matches the in-game item exactly (same type, tier, name, lore, skin) — it was never wrong, just poisoned by the stale duplicate files the 1.0.23 clean build removed.

### 🛡 The jar can never ship stale duplicate files again
- This project lives in iCloud Drive, which keeps resurrecting old `"name 2.json"` conflict copies into the build output (the clean build fixed it once; the next incremental build re-shipped them). The jar task now **excludes any `name <number>.<ext>` file outright**, verified by re-poisoning the build dir and confirming the jar stays clean — so a recipe/advancement/dimension datapack can never be silently broken by them again.

## 1.0.23

### 🛠 Boss crafting recipes actually craft (backpack & co. fixed too)
- **All 5 boss summon tokens now have real crafting recipes** (they only existed as help text before — there were no recipe files, so you could never craft them):
  - **Withering Memory** — coal corners + bone edges around a wither skeleton skull
  - **Gelatinous Crown** — 8 slime blocks around a golden helmet
  - **Boulder Baby** — 8 stone bricks around an iron block
  - **Ominous Eye** — 4 eyes of ender around an ender pearl
  - **Cryogenic Core** — 8 snow blocks around a diamond
- Crafted tokens are **byte-identical to `/ff give` tokens** (same custom data, model, name, lore, glint) — the mod recognizes them and they summon the boss normally.
- **Fixed the recipe-datapack killer**: the jar was shipping **104 stale duplicate files with spaces in their names** (`backpack 2.json`, `wool_to_string 3.json`, `fabric.mod 4.json`, ...) left over in the build output from old copies. Minecraft's `Identifier` rejects spaces, which could poison the whole recipe/advancement/dimension load — that's why the backpack, wool→string, distant memory shard, and everything else "didn't work". A clean build now ships **zero** stale files; all 8 recipes are confirmed in the jar.
- Mod version bumped to 1.0.23 (jar was still 1.0.12 while the changelog had moved on).

## 1.0.22

### 🎒 Expedition chests pay cash — no items inside
- Expedition loot chests no longer hold physical items. Opening one instantly pays a **random cash amount from that expedition's lowest-to-highest range** (Deep Mine $500–$1.5k → Boss Lair $3k–$10k, rich chests pay 3×) into your secured loot, multiplied by the type multiplier as before.
- **Fixes the loot-spill bug**: when a new expedition regenerated a site, the new chest replaced the old one and dropped its contents on the floor. Empty chests mean nothing can ever spill again.
- Chests are marked (Rich Loot Chest / Looted Chest) so each one pays out once; legacy chests from before this update still convert their leftover items to cash.

### 📚 Custom enchant tomes now live in the creative menu
- All **15 custom enchantment tomes** (Chainfire, Sniper, Revolver, Sharpshooter, Recoil, Hunter's Mark, Heavy Bolt, Curse of Fragility, Combo, Berserker + Life Steal, Sticky, Seismic, Mind Wrack, Frostbite) now appear as **grabbable items in the Combat creative tab**, alongside `/ff give` which still works.
- They're NBT-decorated enchanted books (not new item IDs), so they render perfectly for vanilla clients and can't trip the datapack item-model crash.

### 📖 Custom enchant tomes get descriptions (and actually work)
- **Fixed: `/ff give` CC-enchant tomes (Chainfire, Sniper, Revolver, Sharpshooter, Recoil, Hunter's Mark, Heavy Bolt, Fragility, Combo, Berserker) couldn't be fused** — the forge/quick-fuse only recognized the original five tomes, so those books were useless. The forge, quick-fuse shortcut, Item Forge slot, and death-protection now all accept both tome families, and CC enchants apply onto weapons exactly like the originals (I–III, with proper max-level and Tome Saver handling).
- **All custom enchant tomes now show a description** in their lore — `/ff give` books, creative books, and boss drops tell you what the enchant does (e.g. "Fires 4 extra arrows. Can't pair with Infinity.", "Deals +24% damage at long range.").
- Fixed lore refresh bugs that duplicated the Chainfire and Recoil lines when re-applying enchants.
- CC-enchant tomes now have a sell value in shops, consistent with the original tomes.

### 🧭 Fortune & Favors creative tab (client-side)
- New **"Fortune & Favors" creative tab** holding all 15 custom enchant tomes as grabbable items. Brand-new creative tabs aren't part of the server registry sync in 26.2, so the tab is registered client-side — vanilla clients don't see it (they still get the tomes in the Combat tab from the server-side addition).

### 🌙 Day/night cycle no longer snaps back
- The old per-tick watchdog force-re-enabled the daylight and weather cycle game rules every 10 seconds whenever they were off. That fight (with owners, other plugins, or `/time` commands) is what made the clock stutter and snap back after setting night. The mod **never sets the time of day** — the only interference was this watchdog.
- The watchdog is gone. Instead the mod checks the cycle rules **once at server start** and repairs them if they were left off (e.g. legacy state from the old arena-clock freeze), then never touches them again mid-session. Setting night now sticks, and the weather cycle is left alone too.
- Verified the Snow Queen's storm only affects her own realm (weather data is per-dimension) and that all custom dimensions share the overworld clock correctly via the 26.2 timeline system.

## 1.0.21

### 🏠 Claim protection — enforced at the source, fail-closed
- **New `ServerPlayerGameModeMixin`**: claim rules are now enforced directly inside the vanilla break/interact methods, independent of the Fabric event layer. If the event chain is skipped, broken, or silently swallows an error, claimed land is still protected — and on any unexpected error the action is **denied** (fail-closed) instead of allowed.
- The mixin covers breaking, block/fluid placement, and opening containers (non-shop chests, barrels, hoppers...) — shop chests stay openable by customers.
- **PvP is hardened the same way** (`PlayerAttackMixin` on `Player.attack`): attacking a player inside a claim with PvP disabled is cancelled at the source, independent of the event layer.
- **Claim denial messages are throttled** (one per 2 seconds per player) so the event layer and the mixin can both enforce the same rule without spamming chat.

### ⚡ Tab list refreshes instantly
- Equipping/unequipping a title or tag, recoloring, or changing your Title & Tag position now **broadcasts a tab-list update immediately** — other players see the change the moment it happens instead of waiting for the periodic refresh. Wired into the /tags menu and `/tag set|color|clear`, `/ff title`.

## 1.0.20

### 🏷️ Tags & Titles menu — chase what you haven't got yet
- **Locked titles now show as grayed-out cards** in the /tags menu with their unlock condition and live progress (e.g. "Duelist: win 10 duels (currently 4)"), so you can see every title that exists and how close you are.
- **All milestone tags shown with live progress** — "Veteran: day 9/14", "Centurion: day 45/60" — owned tags stay clickable to equip, locked ones show exactly what's left.
- **Menu expanded to 9×6** to fit the full title catalog; the creator's exclusive title stays first.

### ↔️ Title & tag position — your choice
- New per-player **Title & Tag Position** setting (compass card in the /tags menu, cycles Default / Before / After):
  - Default: `[Title] Name [Tag]` (as before)
  - Before: `[Title] [Tag] Name`
  - After: `Name [Title] [Tag]`
- Applies everywhere your name renders — chat and the tab list — and persists per player in `display_prefs.json`.
- **Fixed a latent bug**: `tags.json` was saved but never loaded, so tags/owned tags reset every server restart. It's now loaded at startup.

### 🌙 Day/night cycle spam fixed
- The duel arena used to freeze its clock by turning `doDaylightCycle`/`doWeatherCycle` off — but MC 26.2 game rules are **server-global**, so that froze the overworld's clock too, and the watchdog re-enabled it every 10 seconds, spamming the log. The arena clock freezing is gone (the void arena has constant light, so it never mattered) and the watchdog now warns **once per incident** instead of every 10 seconds.

### ⚔️ UHC duels no longer disable regen for the whole server
- UHC duels used the same global-gamerule trick to disable natural health regen — during any active UHC fight, **nobody on the server regenerated**. Natural regen is now blocked **per player** via `NaturalRegenMixin`: only the fighters in an active UHC duel are affected; everyone else (and spectators) regenerates normally.
