The global `naturalRegeneration` gamerule is never touched anymore.

## 1.0.19

### ⚔ Player raids raid YOU now (no village needed)
- **Bell + Bad Omen works anywhere** — the old "no village close enough" block is gone. Ring a bell with Bad Omen (or use a Raid Banner) and the raiders come hunting the player, wherever you stand.
- **Custom wave system**: 4-6 escalating waves of pillagers → vindicators → ravagers → witches → evokers spawn around the fight and are told to target the player. No vanilla village raid, no villagers, no village raids being hijacked.
- **Custom minibosses at the end**: after the final wave the Warlord + Elder Evoker + Illusioner + Raid Captain + Evoker Lieutenant descend (existing boss bar, participation rewards and speed bonuses all still apply).
- Raids now work underground too — raiders and bosses spawn at the fight's elevation instead of the surface.
- Vanilla villager raids are completely untouched (no minibosses, no suppression).

### 🏟️ Prison: combat zone actually works + zone-aware menu
- **Arena waves auto-spawn**: the arena used to pay out its reward every few seconds and never start wave 2. Now each wave is rewarded exactly once and the next wave drops automatically after 10s, all the way to wave 10.
- **Zone-aware Prison Menu**: opening the star in the combat zone shows sword upgrade + rank up + convert + **Return to Mine** + leave; at the fishing pond it shows the rod + rank up + convert + Return to Mine; in the mine you get the normal mining layout.
- **Sword upgrades**: buy Iron → Diamond → Netherite prison swords (Sharpness scales 1→3, never breaks) with prison cash, from the combat-zone menu.
- **Fishing rod**: entering the pond hands you an enchanted rod (Lure III, Luck of the Sea III, Unbreaking III, unbreakable). Catches pay $30-$80, and 1-in-20 "treasure" catches pay 5x.
- **Return to mine**: a menu button (and chat message) teleports you back to your ore floors from the arena or pond.
- **Map polish**: mine floors now have a full ceiling light grid; the pond got a raised stone rim, corner lantern posts, scattered lily pads, underwater glow and a wooden dock.

### 🎒 Expedition fixes (part 2)
- **Dispensers removed** — no more inventory-loads of arrows and loot scattered everywhere (trap rooms now use pressure plates + TNT only).
- **No more beacons** in treasure rooms (sea lantern markers instead).
- **Mob-den spawners are unbreakable** and properly configured per expedition type — you can't pick them up.
- **Rooms can't seal off**: every chunk now has aligned center doorways so adjacent chunks always line up.
- **Vault Guardian spawns on the entrance pad** — no more suffocating in a wall.
- **Enemies no longer spawn on the roof**: spawn points are picked inside generated maze air.
- **Glowstone exit pad always works** — returning to it escapes instantly, and a rare second exit pad can generate deep in the maze.
- **Rewards nerfed**: mob kill cash halved, chest stacks smaller, mini-boss bonus halved.
- **Mob variants are rare again** (~1 in 16 instead of every spawn) and no name tags anywhere.
- **More rooms**: big halls, pillar halls, mob dens, cache rooms, plus a rare exit pad.

### ⚔ Sword blocking — 1.8 directional
- Blocking now reduces melee damage **only from the front** (the direction you're facing), like 1.8 — back/side hits do full damage.

### 💎 Gem shop
- Phantom membranes replaced by **Repairing Membrane**: put any item or armor in the Item Forge with one and it repairs to full durability. Only sold **1 at a time** per buy (was 4).

### 📋 Dynamic contracts
- Completed jobs no longer count toward the 8-contract cap — done contracts stop blocking new ones.

## 1.0.18

### ⚔ Sword blocking actually works again
- **Blocking is back**: swords lost the `blocks_attacks` component in the 1.0.13 rework, so right-click never entered the blocking state and the 50% reduction never fired. `SwordBlockManager` now re-applies `blocks_attacks` to every held sword on tick (instant block, silent, no durability cost) — the vanilla blocking pose starts on right-click and the damage mixin halves melee hits again.
- **The 50% mixin no longer skips melee**: it bailed out on `source.isDirect()`, which is exactly how every normal melee attack arrives — so blocking reduced nothing. It now reduces direct melee damage while still ignoring projectiles, fall, fire, and drowning.
- **Distant Memory blocking never slows you**: the sword carries `use_effects` (full move speed, sprint allowed) so holding right-click to block keeps you at full speed; existing swords get it re-applied on tick.
- **Attack while blocking**: left-click keeps swinging while holding right-click to block — for the Distant Memory sword and every other blade. Non-sword uses (bows, food) keep vanilla behavior.
- **Gem shop / `/shop` verified elytra-free**: neither the gem shop nor the catalog sells elytra (End-exclusive, phantom membranes only).

## 1.0.17

### ⚔ Player raids — boss bar, 5 bosses, vex reinforcements, participation rewards
- **Boss bar** shows combined HP of all raid bosses during the final phase, with live countdown of remaining bosses
- **5 raid bosses** now spawn on victory instead of 3:
  - §c§lRaid Warlord§7 (Vindicator, 150 HP, netherite armor, melee)
  - §5§lElder Evoker§7 (Evoker, 120 HP, summons vex reinforcements every 20s)
  - §9§lRaid Illusioner§7 (Illusioner, 100 HP, creates clones, bow user)
  - §6§lRaid Captain§7 (Pillager, 90 HP, drops the special Raid Crossbow)
  - §5§lRaid Evoker Lt§7 (Evoker, 100 HP, standard caster lieutenant)
- **Elder Evoker** summons 2-3 vexes every 20 seconds with limited life (30s each)
- **Raid Captain drops a special crossbow**: Quick Charge III + Multishot + Unbreaking III + Mending, named "⚔ Raid Crossbow"
- **Participation tracking**: players who fight in more raid waves get up to +50% bonus cash at victory
  - Participation counted every 5 seconds near the raid center during active waves
  - Message shows your participation bonus percentage at victory
- Boss bar updates in real time with combined HP and remaining boss count
- All boss death VFX preserved and enhanced

## 1.0.16

### ⚔ Player raids reworked — dramatic VFX and multiple mini-bosses
- **Three bosses spawn on victory** instead of just the Warlord:
  - §c§lRaid Warlord§7 (Vindicator, 120 HP, netherite armor, melee)
  - §5§lRaid Evoker§7 (Evoker lieutenant, 100 HP, caster fangs + summons)
  - §6§lRaid Captain§7 (Pillager lieutenant, 80 HP, crossbow, iron armor)
- All three must be slain to claim the victory reward
- **Dramatic spawn VFX**: portal ring (60 particles), soul fire flames, large smoke, ender dragon growl + wither spawn + raid horn sounds
- **Wave tracking**: each new wave triggers a raid horn sound + soul fire/smoke particles + chat announcement
- **Ambient VFX**: crimson spores swirl around the raid center every 3 seconds, campfire smoke from the ground, angry villager particles on nearby players
- **Raid horn** plays every 30 seconds during active raids
- **Warlord death VFX**: end rod burst (30 particles), totem of undying (20), firework (15), explosion + challenge complete sounds
- **Lieutenant death VFX**: end rod (15) + soul fire flame (10) + soul escape sound
- **Victory VFX**: firework (50), totem (30), end rod (25), happy villager (20), challenge complete + firework blast sounds
- **Defeat VFX**: large smoke (40), smoke (30), ender dragon death + wither death sounds
- All raid announcements now have decorative border lines

## 1.0.15

### ⚡ Events reworked — dramatic visuals and 3 new event types
- **Gold Rush**: ore blocks drop double items for 10 minutes
- **Thunderstorm**: lightning strikes + electric particles engulf the world
- **Double Trouble**: double mob spawns, double damage taken, double kill cash
- Dramatic startup: ender dragon growl + wither spawn sounds, particle bursts per event type
- Ongoing particle effects every 1.5s on every player during active events
- Dramatic ending: firework particles + challenge complete sound + decorative borders
- Warning particles during the last 10 seconds before an event ends
- `/ff event` now lists all 7 event types

### 🏟️ Prison combat arena
- New combat arena room at the prison spawn platform with iron bar walls and glowstone floor
- Wave-based survival: each wave spawns more mobs with scaling HP and armor
- Cash rewards: 100 × wave² prison cash per wave cleared (wave 1 = $100, wave 5 = $2,500)
- Mobs scale with wave: zombies, skeletons, spiders, pillagers, witches
- Auto-progresses through 10 waves, then resets

### 🧳 Expedition wandering supplier
- A villager NPC spawns every ~3 minutes inside expeditions
- Drops emergency supplies (bread, torches) on the ground nearby
- Named "Expedition Supplier" — right-click to interact

### 👹 Expedition mini-bosses
- Mini-bosses spawn when the player is deep in the expedition (chunk radius ≥ 10)
- Type-specific bosses: Broodmother (Deep Mine), Undying Champion (Monster Cave), Crystal Warden (Crystal Cavern), Void Reaper (Void), Vault Breaker (Boss Lair)
- 5x HP, full iron armor, tier-5 rare mob variant (glowing)
- Bonus reward: $5,000-$15,000+ loot value on kill (scaled by expedition multiplier)

### 💰 Expedition rewards balanced
- Mob kill cash increased 4x: zombies $200, skeletons $250, creepers $400, blazes $600, wither skeletons $800
- Chest loot doubled: gold ingots 4-12 per chest, emeralds 2-7, rich chests get 2-5 diamonds
- Expedition multiplier (1.5x-4.0x) still applies on top of all rewards at extraction

## 1.0.14

### 🏛️ Expeditions reworked — backrooms-style endless generation
- **All expeditions now take place in the expedition dimension** (including Void — no longer in the Nether)
- **Backrooms-style procedural generation**: dungeon chunks generate as you approach the frontier, creating an ever-expanding maze that feels endless
- Each chunk is 8×8 blocks with doorways punched through walls — corridors connect infinitely
- **Loot chests** scattered throughout: treasure rooms (rich chests + beacon), loot chest rooms (1-2 chests), and cache rooms (extra ore + chest)
- Chest loot includes diamonds, gold, emeralds, ender pearls, blaze rods, golden apples, netherite scraps (Void), and more
- **Trap rooms**: pressure plates triggering arrow dispensers, TNT pitfalls with tripwires
- **Mob kills give cash**: zombies ($50), skeletons ($60), creepers ($100), blazes ($150), wither skeletons ($200), vindicators ($180), and more — scaled by expedition multiplier
- **Unbreakable boundaries**: floor, walls, and ceiling cannot be broken to escape the expedition
- Type-specific generation: stone (Deep Mine), cobblestone (Monster Cave), amethyst (Crystal Cavern), obsidian+blackstone (Void), deepslate (Boss Lair)
- Type-specific ores: nether quartz/gold (Void), amethyst/diamond (Crystal Cavern), gold/diamond/emerald (Boss Lair)

### 🏥 Prison rework
- **3x cheaper upgrades**: pickaxe upgrades start at $1,500 (was $5,000), rank-ups start at $160 (was $500)
- **Start with iron pickaxe** (was stone) — can mine iron and coal immediately
- **Enchantment upgrades**: Efficiency I→V, Fortune I→III (from level 3), Unbreaking (from level 3)
- **Better pickaxe progression**: Iron → Diamond (tier 3+) → Netherite (tier 6+)
- **Mob spawners** on each mine floor — zombies spawn for combat
- **Mob kill rewards**: $10-$2,000 prison cash per kill depending on mob type
- **Fishing rewards**: $15-$40 per catch while in prison
- **Expanded prison menu** (4 rows): mining, combat, and fishing activity info

### 🐉 Dupe glitch fix — dragon egg teleport blocked
- Created `DragonEggBlockMixin` that cancels vanilla dragon egg teleport behavior (the main dupe vector)
- Audited grave claim system, hopper extraction, and item drop mixins — all secure

### 🕐 All times changed from UTC to EST
- Daily/weekly challenge resets, newspaper publishing, leaderboard resets, and daily streak resets all use EST (America/New_York) now
- All user-facing text updated from "midnight UTC" to "midnight EST"

## 1.0.13

### ⚔ Sword blocking — 50% damage, directional, no projectile blocking
- Sword blocking now reduces melee damage by **50%** (not 100%). Removed vanilla `blocks_attacks` component entirely.
- Still takes knockback and shows hurt animation. Does NOT block projectiles.
- **Distant Memory sword**: 5 damage, 1025 attack speed, can attack while blocking.

### 💎 Gems + Gem Shop
- **Gems** are a new balance-based currency separate from favor tokens. Shown in /balance.
- Earned from raids, challenges, contracts, daily streaks, achievements.
- **`/gemshop`** command + menu hub button — buy exclusive items: mystery keys, raid banners, elytra, dragon eggs, totems, netherite, and more!
- Favor tokens remain as physical paper items (unchanged).

### 💼 Jobs pay 5x more
- All job rewards multiplied by 5x. Elder guardian: $600→$3,000. Zombie: $115→$575. Diamond ore: $300→$1,500.

### 📋 Challenges menu fix
- Challenges menu now loads the challenge pool before opening, fixing the "won't open" bug.

### 📦 /deliver hopper fix
- Closing the delivery menu with items in the hopper slot now returns them to inventory. If inventory is full, the menu stays open with a warning instead of dropping items.

### 🔧 /ff restore reworked
- `/ff restore [player]` now restores the player's **last pre-death inventory** (admin only). Snapshots are taken before every death and persisted to disk.

### ✨ Mythic mobs glow
- Mythic (tier 5) rare mob variants are now always visible and glowing.

### ⛏ Prison reworked — cheaper, more fun, more to do
- **Upgrades 3x cheaper**: pickaxe upgrade $5k→$1.5k at Lv1, rank-up $500→$160 at rank A.
- **Start with iron pickaxe** (was stone) — can actually mine iron and coal from rank A.
- **Better enchantment progression**: Efficiency I→V, Fortune I→III, Unbreaking at Lv3+.
- **Mob spawners** added to mine floors (tier 1+) — kill mobs for prison cash!
- **Mob kill rewards**: zombie $20, creeper $35, enderman $50, ravager $150, warden $2000.
- **Fishing rewards**: $15-$40 prison cash per catch in prison.
- **Expanded prison menu** (4 rows): sell, rank up, pickaxe upgrade, convert, combat/fishing/mining info, leave.
- Max saturation, Nether Star in slot 9, block sell prices 5x.

### 🏰 Expedition improvements
- Mazes expanded to 12x12 with treasure rooms, trap rooms, cache rooms, and two exits.

### 🌍 Other
- Grave heads preserve player skins. Expedition mazes expanded with rooms.
- **All time displays changed from UTC to EST** (America/New_York timezone for proper DST handling).
- **Dragon egg dupe glitch fixed** — the vanilla onPlace teleport that lets players duplicate dragon eggs is now blocked.

## 1.0.12

## 1.0.12

### 👟 Slime Boots bounce toggle
- **Shift-right-click** the Slime Boots to toggle the bounce itself: ON (spring back on landing) or OFF (land flat, no fall damage) - no more sneaking every time you want a flat landing. Right-click still cycles jump boost (Off/I/II/III), and sneaking on landing still overrides the bounce.
- Lore updated to explain the new toggle.

### 🤫 Hidden elevators stay hidden
- If a carpet sits on top of an elevator (secret base style), the teleport no longer fires the portal particles or the enderman teleport sound - the floor looks completely normal while still working.

## 1.0.11

### ⚔ Sword blocking — server-side, consistent, 50%
- Ported the **Sword Blocking datapack** (Insperation folder) into the mod as pure Java: every sword you hold now gets the vanilla `blocks_attacks` component, so **all swords block everywhere** — same behavior always, not just in legacy duels. The component is re-applied on tick, so it survives syncs and newly-picked-up swords.
- **Incoming damage reduced by a flat 50%** (base 0, factor 0.5 — the datapack's 0.9/0.9 blocked ~everything; you asked for half). Instant block, never disabled by axe hits, shield block/break sounds, 1 durability per blocked hit.
- **You can block and swing**: holding right-click blocks, left-click keeps attacking — works for the Distant Memory sword and every other blade. The old 50% mixin now stands down when the component is present, so there's no double-reduction.

### 🔔 Datapacks verified wired
- **bell_find_raiders**: was only glowing raiders within 96 blocks; now glows **every** raider in the dimension for 3s (matching the "bell finds all raiders" datapack) — already executed from the Java bell hook on ring.
- Orbital Strike Cannon (rods + tick loop), raid_victory (Warlord fanfare), and the sword blocking above are all bundled and live. elytra-lunge and the sword-enchantment compat are already in the mod.

### ⛏ Elevator + carpet fix
- Elevators now work with a carpet on top: standing on a carpet-covered elevator still triggers it, and a carpet-topped elevator is a valid destination.

### 💰 `/spawners sellall`
- Sells **every spawner-loot item in your inventory at once**, including each item's level multiplier (`/spawners sell` works too). Pays straight to your balance with a count + total.

### 🏠 Claims GUI (`/claim`, `/claim menu`, /menu → Claims & Land)
- The claim system finally has a proper GUI. `/claim` and `/claim menu` open **Claims & Land**, a framed overview showing every claim you own (position, size, admin count) — click any claim to jump straight into its permission screen.
- Quick-action row: **Get Claimer**, **Claim Loot** (collect auction/chest-shop/boss payouts), **Claim Slots** (used/max + buy next slot with cost shown), **Claim border** (Full/Small/Off cycle), **Manage ALL claims** (one screen, whole-base policy), and **Recover claims** (OP only).
- Standing inside a claim, the GUI shows a banner for it with a shortcut to manage it. `/claim give` still hands out a Chunk Claimer directly for the old behavior.
- The /menu hub's "Claims & Land" button now opens the GUI instead of just silently dropping a claimer on you.
- **Back navigation**: the claim permission screen now has a BACK button that returns to the Claims & Land overview, and the overview has a BACK button that returns to the /menu hub.

## 1.0.10

### 🐛 Hotfix: duel arena no longer blocks world load
- The `fortuneandfavors:duel_arena` dimension type used the pre-26.2 schema (it was missing the now-required `has_ender_dragon_fight` key plus the new `attributes`/`default_clock`/`timelines` structure). That made the whole datapack fail registry loading, so **worlds wouldn't open** ("Failed to load level data or datapacks") — the world itself was never corrupted.
- Rewritten against the vanilla 26.2 overworld dimension type (bright ambient light, no dragon fight, no mob spawning), and the mod version is bumped to 1.0.10 so the jar filename finally matches the changelog.
- **`minecraft:exclusive_set/damage` now truly empty** — it was missing `replace: true`, so the vanilla entries (Sharpness/Smite/Bane/Impaling/Density/Breach) silently merged back in and the damage enchantments stayed mutually exclusive. Audited all 13 bundled JSONs against the 26.2 codecs (enchantments, recipes, tags, dimensions) — everything else checks out.

### ⚔ Player Raids — cooldown + opt-in banner
- **Raids can no longer chain endlessly**: each dimension shares a **2-hour raid cooldown** that starts when a raid is won, lost, or expires. The cooldown persists across restarts (`player_raids.json`).
- **New opt-in item: the 🚩 Raid Banner** — right-click it near a village to ignite a Player Raid on demand (consumed on use, dropped by slain Raid Warlords, and available via `/ff give raidbanner`). It shows an error if raids are on cooldown or there's no village close enough.

## 1.0.9

### Bug fixes
- **"First Mythic Spawn" record no longer says "the server"** — it credits the nearest player who saw it spawn (falls back to "a wandering adventurer").
- **Elite/Ancient/Mythic mobs no longer glow through walls** — the permanent glowing effect is gone from variant mobs entirely, which also takes a load off the server and client.
- **Special enemy variants are now actually rare** — spawn rates went from ~50% of all hostile mobs down to 15% total (Armored 10%, Cursed 3.5%, Elite 1.2%, Ancient 0.2%, Mythic 0.1%).
- **UHC Duel now uses legacy 1.8 combat by default** (picking UHC from the Legacy menu, or directly, gives you 1.8-style combat; only the Modern submenu picks modern).
- **"The arena refused to open" fixed** — the duel realm dimension files were missing entirely (empty folders), so the arena world never loaded. The `fortuneandfavors:duel_arena` dimension + type are now bundled.
- **Orbital strike rods (`/ff give nuke` etc.) actually appear and fire** — the rods' NBT/names now match the bundled datapack exactly (`Orbital_Cannon:1010` + exact names), `/ff give` hands them to you directly instead of relying on a function call, and the datapack's tick loop is wired into `minecraft:tick` so thrown rods detonate.
- **Sword blocking now works while HOLDING right-click** — the block window was 0.5s (tap-only); it now stays active until you release, for legacy duels and the Distant Memory sword.
- **New `/ff restore <player>` (OP)** — returns every unclaimed grave item straight to that player's inventory (works with no argument for yourself). It was missing entirely before.

### 🔮 Two new runes + rune GUI
- **Rune of Swiftness** (+5% movement speed while held) and **Rune of Frost** (25% chance to slow enemies on hit) join the family, and drop from Mystery Chests (`/ff give runefrost` / `runeswiftness`).
- New **`/runes` GUI**: shows your held weapon's socketed runes and all five rune types with your inventory count — click to socket straight from the menu.

### 🎒 Expeditions
- **Risk meter**: the more loot you secure, the deadlier the mobs that spawn (tiers scale 1-3 with heat). At high heat a **Mythic Hunter** can drop in to hunt you.
- **New 5th expedition: 👑 Boss Lair** — a vault guardian seals the exit; you can't escape until you slay it, then the 4x cash-out opens. Found in `/expedition`.

### ⚔ Player Raids
- When a village raid is won, the **Raid Warlord** (a netherite-armored Ancient miniboss) descends holding the treasure — the victory payout now lands when the Warlord is slain, and the slayer gets broadcast across the server.

## 1.0.8

### Bug fixes
- **Excalibur now actually one-shots.** The damage/strength mixins checked for a `✧`-star name, but the item is created with a `✦`-star — so the check never matched. It now matches any Excalibur name.
- **Sculk Sensor Leggings no longer run on their own.** Vibration Sense now starts OFF, and only a shift-right-click toggles it (plain right-clicks while wearing used to flip it on/off accidentally).
- **Explosion rebuild now really pulls you out.** The crater rescue used to run once per explosion at the first rebuild tick and only checked blocks you were *inside*; it now runs every half-second while rebuilds are pending and also lifts anyone standing at or below the top of a column being rebuilt — you get TP'd to the top.

### ⚠ Dangerous Mining Zones
- The server rolls 1-2 dangerous mining zones in deepslate caves near spawn each start: ores mined there pay bonus cash and can double-drop, but stronger tiered mobs spawn inside and red smoke marks the border. Entering warns you with a "DANGEROUS MINING AREA" actionbar. Sub-chunk sized - never a whole chunk.

### 🎒 Expeditions (`/expedition`, `/menu → Expeditions`)
- Four expeditions: ⛏ **Deep Mine**, 👹 **Monster Cave** (diamond-armored undead hordes), 💎 **Crystal Cavern**, 🌑 **Void Expedition** (Nether).
- Each teleports you into a dangerous pocket with a time limit (10-16 min). Ores you mine go into a temporary expedition bag - leave the zone alive to cash out at the type's multiplier (1.5x-3x), or **die / run out of time and you lose everything**.
- Running out of time triggers a collapse: smoke, damage, and for Monster Cave a full zombie swarm. Escaping broadcasts your haul to the server.

### 🔮 Runes
- Three socketable upgrades for swords and axes: ⚡ **Rune of Haste** (+5% attack speed), 🔥 **Rune of Flame** (25% chance to ignite on hit), 💎 **Rune of Fortune** (20% chance for bonus cash + XP per kill).
- Hold a sword/axe in your main hand and the rune in your offhand, then right-click to fuse it in. Runes drop from Rare/Epic/Legendary Mystery Chests (and `/ff give rune`).

### ✨ Persistent Cosmetics (`/cosmetics`, `/menu → Cosmetics`)
- Titles, name colors and particle trails (flame, sparkle, hearts, ender, cloud) that drop from **Epic and Legendary Mystery Chests** and persist across resets in `cosmetics.json`.
- Equip them in the new Cosmetics GUI - titles and name colors show in the tab list, trails follow you around.

### 🏆 Weekly Leaderboard (`/leaderboard`, `/lb`, `/menu → Leaderboard`)
- Four categories: Richest Players, Boss Slayers, Bounty Hunters, Hard Workers (jobs).
- Every Monday 00:00 UTC the top 3 of each category are announced and paid **$50k / $25k / $10k**, then the week resets. The GUI shows current standings, your rank, and time until reset.

### Menu Hub
- Three new buttons: **Expeditions**, **Cosmetics**, and **Leaderboard** (now 22 features in one window).

## 1.0.7

### Menu Hub rebuilt (`/menu`)
- Menu expanded to a 9x5 window with every feature in one place: Shop, Sell, Auction, Jobs, Tokens, Skills, Claims, Raid Bosses, Duels, Bounty, Guild, Deliveries, Mystery Chests, Server News, Daily Streak, Boss Codex, Challenges, First Ever Records, Tags & Titles, and Trading.
- New GUIs behind the buttons: `NewsMenu` (today's paper), `CodexMenu` (boss kill histories), `StreakMenu` (login streak, duel wins, boss streak, bounty level, titles), `ChallengesMenu` (daily & weekly progress bars), `RecordsMenu` (first-ever hall of fame), `TagsMenu` (tag/title/records).

### Server Newspaper (`/news`)
- 45+ headline templates across four sections (Top Story, Economy, Bounty Board, Discovery) so the daily paper is never the same twice.
- Real data still wins: boss kills, richest player, active bounties, guild wars, and first-ever records are mixed into the flavor stories.
- `/news` now opens the newspaper GUI (and publishes if today's edition hasn't dropped yet).

### Jobs (`/jobs`)
- Five jobs per hour instead of three.
- New job types: **Chop** (logs, with an axe) and **Farm** (crops, with a hoe) alongside Mine and Kill; bigger template pools for all four.
- Trade jobs can now ask for 3 trades.
- Job Board GUI rebuilt: progress bars, claimed/strikethrough states, glowing claim cards.
- Actionbar nudge when a job completes mid-game, and a golden particle celebration + level-up sound when you claim one.

### Guild Mine → `/mine`
- The mine is now a full GUI: `/mine` (or `/guild mine`) opens it with live stock, progress to the $10k payout, production rate, and payout ETA.
- Build/Upgrade button (founder only) with the exact cost shown - the old `/guild mine` text command could never actually build the mine, that's fixed.
- Deposit buttons ($1k / $10k / $100k) plus a custom amount type-in prompt (`/mine <amount>` still works).
- Upgrades no longer wipe your deposited stock.

### `/changelog`
- New command that prints the bundled changelog in chat (kept in sync with CHANGELOG.md).

### Daily Rewards GUI (`/rewards`)
- Daily login rewards moved from auto-granted chat messages into a proper calendar GUI: Day 1/3/7/14/30 milestones, current streak, freezes, and a big "Claim" button with a golden celebration VFX.
- Rewards are now claimed instead of auto-granted - you get a chat nudge on login when one is waiting. Reachable from `/rewards`, `/daily`, or /menu → Daily Streak.

### /ff help rebuilt
- Help overview now splits into **For everyone** and **For admins (OP)** sections (the admin block only shows to OPs).
- New topics for every feature: menu, guilds & wars & the mine, streaks & titles, challenges, codex, records & tags, newspaper, deliveries/contracts, mystery chests, duels, disasters, rare mobs, changelog - plus OP topics: config, economy/staff, and world tools.

### Deliveries & Dynamic Contracts
- Up to **8 contracts** can be live at once (shortages + urgent contracts share the cap).
- Shortages now roll in every **4 hours** instead of once a day, so the market stays busy; shortage pool grew to 15 items.
- **Fixed the "dropped items" bug**: closing the delivery window returns hopper items to your inventory - if your inventory is full it keeps the window open and warns you instead of dropping items on the floor. Hopper contents also survive window refreshes now.
- The Job Board now shows live dynamic contracts with a shortcut to the delivery window, so "dynamic jobs" are visible in-game.

### GUI polish pass
- Consistent colored-glass frames across the menu hub, jobs, daily rewards, challenges, tags, records, news, codex, streaks, bounty board, guild mine, mystery chests, and the raid boss help.
- **Bounty Board**: real player-head skins for online targets, online/offline status.
- **First Ever Records**: themed icons per record (boss skulls, gold block for the first millionaire, nether star for the first mythic) and a record count.
- **Daily Rewards**: 7-day rolling calendar with today/milestone/past/future states.
- **Tags & Titles**: shows owned milestone tags as clickable cards - equip `[Veteran]` and future tags from the GUI (owned tags are persisted; day-14 now unlocks it permanently).
- **Mystery Chests**: upgraded to a framed 9x4 window with your key count, per-tier "have key" states, and a celebration burst when a chest opens.
- **Mystery Boxes**: reveal kept its rainbow ring, plus the shared celebration effect.

### Mines → multiple tiers
- The guild mine is now five named mines from cheap to expensive: **Coal → Iron → Gold → Diamond → Netherite Shafts** (Lv 1-5). The `/mine` GUI shows every tier with cost and production; click the next tier to build/upgrade it.

### Jobs - way more
- Mine pool grew 20 → 35, Kill pool 20 → 36, plus more Chop and Farm templates (crimson/warped stems, sweet berries, bamboo, kelp, drip leaves...).

### First-join welcome GUI
- New players get a one-time welcome screen with buttons straight to the menu hub, shop, jobs, daily rewards, and the wiki.

### World events are admin-triggerable
- `/ff event <blood_moon|mining_collapse|merchant_festival|monster_invasion>` (OP) starts an event on demand, `/ff event stop` ends it, `/ff event list` shows them, and `/ff event mythic` spawns a Mythic mob nearby for testing.

### Celebration VFX
- Job/reward claims now fire an expanding golden shockwave ring, sparkle orbit, note bursts, fireworks, and a mid-burst golden rain.

## 1.0.6

### Critical Fixes
- **1.8 combat 1-dmg bug fixed** — `LegacySweepMixin` rewritten. The old mixin wrapped `getAttackStrengthScale(0.5F)` and returned 0 to suppress sweep, but that return value also gates main-hit damage, causing the 1-dmg bare-hand punch. The new approach lets the damage scale run naturally (the +100 attack speed modifier already makes it 1.0) and instead suppresses sweep by returning an empty entity list from `getEntitiesOfClass` during legacy duels.
- **Anvil "Too Expensive" removed** — `AnvilMixin` caps repair cost at 39 (the vanilla hard cap) so items can always be combined as long as you have the XP.
- **Mining pity system** — `MiningPity`: breaking naturally-generated stone (3%: raw iron/copper/redstone/lapis), deepslate (4%: raw iron/gold/diamond/emerald), tuff (2%: raw iron), or netherrack (3%: gold nuggets/quartz/netherite scrap) has a chance to drop bonus raw ores.
- **Better multiplayer sleep** — `BetterSleepMixin`: only 50% of online players need to sleep to skip the night (rounds up, minimum 1).

### Datapack Additions
- `damage.json` exclusive_set emptied — Sharpness, Smite and Bane of Arthropods can now coexist on the same item.
- `wool_to_string` recipe — white wool crafts into 4 string.
- `boltbringer` enchantment registered in `data/fortuneandfavors/enchantment/` with its own exclusive_set tag and `ff_damage_weapons` item tag.
- Enchantment compat files for `sharpness`, `smite`, `bane_of_arthropods` (supported items / exclusivity).
- `bell_find_raiders` function — ringing a bell reveals all nearby raiders.
- `raid_victory` function — raid victory cleanup/announcement hook.

### New Manager: Streak Tracker (`streaks.json`)
- Duel win streaks with milestones at 10 ("Duelist"), 25 ("Swordsman"), 50 ("Champion").
- Boss kill streaks (4 kills without dying) → "Boss Slayer" title.
- Bounty Hunter XP — 1 level per 5 bounties, level 20 → "Bounty Hunter" title.
- Titles display in chat and the tab list via `TitleManager`.

### Phase 1 — Daily Systems
- **Daily Login Streak** — midnight UTC reset, streak progression, streak freezes (earned every 5 days, max 3, auto-used if you miss exactly 1 day). Milestones: Day 1 welcome, Day 3 $500, Day 7 Common Mystery Key + $5k, Day 14 "Veteran" tag + $15k, Day 30 "Dedicated" title + $50k.
- **Dynamic Contracts** — urgent contracts (3+ players die to a boss → kill-it quest, $50k), shortage contracts (random resource shortages from a pool of 10 items), auto-broadcast, 24h expiry, persisted to `contracts.json`.
- **Deliver Menu + `/deliver`** — 4-row GUI with the contract list, a hopper slot, and smart item matching against active contracts.
- **Menu Hub expanded** — new buttons for Deliver, Daily Streak, Custom Tags, Boss Codex, Dynamic Contracts, Mystery Chests, Server Newspaper.

### Phase 2 — World Dangers
- **Server Disasters** — Blood Moon, Mining Collapse, Merchant Festival, Monster Invasion; 5% chance every 30 min, 10 min duration, 30 min cooldown, persisted to disk.
- **Rare Mob Variants** — 6 tiers (Normal → Armored → Cursed → Elite → Ancient → Mythic) with weighted spawn rolls, stat multipliers, custom names, glow for Elite+, and rare drops per tier.
- **Boss Codex** — per-boss profiles: first discovered, first defeated, total kills, fastest kill (with player name), deaths caused.
- **First Ever Records** — permanent historical records (first boss kill, first millionaire, first mythic, etc.) announced globally.

### Phase 3 — Goals & Cooperation
- **Cooperative Achievements** — 8 achievements (United, Wolfpack, Flawless, Speed Demon, Unbreakable, Server Legend, First Million, Bounty Hunter) with cash/token/tag rewards.
- **Daily & Weekly Challenges** — 9 daily + 5 weekly templates, auto-rotation at midnight / start of week, progress tracked per player.

### Phase 4 — Social Features
- **Server Newspaper** — auto-published at midnight UTC: Top Story (boss kills), Economy (richest player), Bounty Board, Discovery.
- **Guild Wars** — `/guild declarewar <guild>` and `/guild peace <guild>`, enemy particles (angry villager above enemy players, visible only to the opposing guild), war kills tracked with double PvP score.
- **`/tags` command** — shows First Ever records and your tag.

### Phase 5 — Economy
- **Mystery Chests** — 4 tiers (Common/Rare/Epic/Legendary), key system, weighted loot tables, cash + item rewards, `/menu → Mystery Chests`.
- **Guild Mine** — 5 upgrade levels ($100k → $50M), periodic resource production scaling with level, `/guild mine` and `/guild mine <amount>`.

### Config, Sync & Polish
- **OneConfig per-server settings now sync** — server setting changes are broadcast to every online player so Cloth Config and OneConfig screens stay in sync, OneConfig server switches push changes to the server immediately, and the config manager no longer crashes when OneConfig isn't installed.
- **Sculk abilities are togglable** — new "Sculk abilities" feature switch (config file, config menu, Cloth Config, OneConfig) that gates the Sculk Sensor Leggings' vibration sense and its shift-click toggle.
- **Trading UI modernized** — player heads with names replace the plain panes, a live trade-value readout ("You're ahead / You're behind" with item + cash breakdown), cleaner accept states, and a summary book.
- **Combat balance** — TNT explosions deal +50% damage, ender pearl teleport damage is reduced to 60%, and axes used in player-vs-player combat take extra durability.
- **Elytra Lunge** — boost while gliding (look-direction dash with a 2.5s cooldown) via `ElytraLunge`.
